  "content": "\"\"\"Add collaborative album design tables for real-time album editing.\n\nRevision ID: 0188\nRevises: 0187\nCreate Date: 2026-02-01\n\nThis migration adds tables for:\n- albums: Master ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Album Templates Table - Predefined layouts for different album types\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    op.execute(\n        \"\"\"\n        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS album_templates (\n            template_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n            workspace_id UUID REFERENCES workspaces(workspace_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n            name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,\n            description TEXT,\n            album_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CHECK (album_type IN ('wedding', 'portrait', 'event', 'family', 'newborn', 'boudoir', 'commercial', 'custom')),\n            category VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'standard' CHECK (category IN ('standard', 'premium', 'custom')),\n            page_size JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{\"width\": 12, \"height\": 12, \"unit\": \"inch\"}'::jsonb,\n            default_spread_layout JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{\"type\": \"single\", \"columns\": 1}'::jsonb,\n            spread_templates JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,\n            cover_options JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n            spine_options JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n            color_palette JSONB DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,\n            typography JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n            thumbnail_url TEXT,\n            is_system BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,\n            is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,\n            sort_order INTEGER DEFAULT 0,\n            created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),\n            updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()\n        );\n        \"\"\"\n    )\n\n    # Indexes for album_templates\n    op.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_album_templates_workspace ON album_templates(workspace_id);\")\n    op.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_album_templates_type ON album_templates(album_type);\")\n    op.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_album_templates_system ON album_templates(is_system, is_active);\")\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Albums Table - Master album entity\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    op.execute(\n        \"\"\"\n        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS albums (\n            album_id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n            workspace_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES workspaces(workspace_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n            gallery_id UUID REFERENCES galleries(gallery_id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n            template_id UUID REFERENCES album_templates(template_id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\n            title VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,\n            description VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'in_review', 'approved', 'ordered', 'archived')),\n            page_size JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{\"width\": 12, \"height\": 12, \"unit\": \"inch\"}'::jsonb,\n            page_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n            cover_config JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n            spine_config JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n            design_config JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n            metadata JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,\n            current_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n            last_edited_by UUID REFERENCES \"\"\"\n    )\n\n    # Indexes for albums\n    op.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_albums_workspace ON albums(workspace_id);\")\n    op.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_albums_gallery albums(status);\")\n    op.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_albums_type ON albums(album_type);\")\n    op.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_albums_created ON albums(workspace_id, created_at DESC);\")\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Album Spreads Table - Individual pages/spreads\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    op.execute(\n        \"\"\"\n        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS album_spreads (\n            spread_id UUID PRIMARY KEY    # Album Comments Table - Comments with @mentions\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    op.execute(\n        \"\"\"\n        CREATE TABLE IF NOT concurrent editing, presence tracking, and version history.\n\nImplements:\n- CRDT-based operation transformation for concurrent edits\n- Lamport timestamps for causal ordering\n- Vector clocks for detecting concurrent operations\n- Real-time presence and cursor tracking\n- Version snapshots for rollback\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport \"element:delete\"\n    ELEMENT_MOVE = \"element:move\"\n    ELEMENT_RESIZE = \"element:resize\"\n    ELEMENT_ROTATE = \"element:rotate\"\n    ELEMENT_STYLE = \"element:style\"\n    ELEMENT_REORDER = \"comment:update\"\n    COMMENT_DELETE = \"comment:delete\"\n    COMMENT_RESOLVE = \"comment:resolve\"\n\n    # Presence\n    CURSOR_MOVE = \"cursor:move\"\n    SELECTION_CHANGE = \"selection:change\"\n    SPREAD_VIEW = \"spread:view\"\n\n\nclass AlbumSessionStatus(str, Enum):\n    ACTIVE = \"active\"\n    IDLE = \"idle\"\n    DISCONNECTED = \"disconnected\"\n    EXPIRED = \"expired\"\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Constants\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nALBUM_SESSION_PREFIX = \"album:session:\"\nALBUM_PRESENCE_PREFIX = \"album:presence:\"\nALBUM_CURSOR_PREFIX = \"#BB8FCE\", \"#85C1E9\",\n    \"#F8B500\", \"#00D4AA\", \"#FF7675\", \"#74B9FF\", \"#A29BFE\",\n]\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# CRDT Implementation - Last Writer Wins Register with Lamport Timestamps\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass LWWRegister:\n    \"\"\"Last Writer Wins Register for CRDT-based conflict resolution.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, value: Any = None, timestamp: int = 0, writer_id: str = \"\"):\n        self.value = value\n        self.timestamp = timestamp\n        self.writer_id = writer_id\n\n    def update(self, value: Any, timestamp: int, writer_id: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Update the register if the new timestamp is greater.\n\n        Returns True if the update was applied.\n        \"\"\"\n        if timestamp > self.timestamp or (timestamp == self.timestamp and writer_id > self.writer_id):\n            self.value = value\n            self.timestamp = timestamp\n            self.writer_id = writer_id\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    def merge(self, other: \"LWWRegister\") -> \"LWWRegister\":\n        \"\"\"Merge with another LWW register.\"\"\"\n        if other.timestamp > self.timestamp or (other.timestamp == self.timestamp and other.writer_id > self.writer_id):\n            return LWWRegister(other.value, other.timestamp, other.writer_id)\n        return LWWRegister(self.value, self.timestamp, self.writer_id)\n\n    def to_dict(self) -> dict:\n        return {\n            \"value\": self.value,\n            \"timestamp\": self.timestamp,\n            \"writer_id\": self.writer_id,\n        }\n\n    @classmethod\n    def from_dict(cls, data: dict) -> \"LWWRegister\":\n        return cls(\n            value=data.get(\"value\"),\n            timestamp=data.get(\"timestamp\", 0),\n            writer_id=data.get(\"writer_id\", \"\"),\n        )\n\n\nclass VectorClock:\n    \"\"\"Vector clock for tracking causal ordering of events.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, clock: dict[str, int] | None = None):\n        self.clock = clock or {}\n\n    def increment(self, node_id: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Increment the counter for a node.\"\"\"\n        self.clock[node_id] = self.clock.get(node_id, 0) + 1\n\n    def get(self, node_id: str) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Get the counter for a node.\"\"\"\n        return self.clock.get(node_id, 0)\n\n    def merge(self, other: \"VectorClock\") -> \"VectorClock\":\n        \"\"\"Merge with another vector clock (take max of each).\"\"\"\n        all_nodes = set(self.clock.keys()) | set(other.clock.keys())\n        merged = {}\n        for node in all_nodes:\n            merged[node] = max(self.clock.get(node, 0), other.clock.get(node, 0))\n        return VectorClock(merged)\n\n    def is_concurrent(self, other: \"VectorClock\") -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if two clocks are concurrent (neither happened before the other).\"\"\"\n        self_before = False\n        other_before = False\n\n        all_nodes = set(self.clock.keys()) | set(other.clock.keys())\n        for node in all_nodes:\n            s = self.clock.get(node, 0)\n            o = other.clock.get(node, 0)\n            if s < o:\n                other_before = True\n            if s > o:\n                self_before = True\n\n        return self_before and other_before\n\n    def to_dict(self) -> dict:\n        return dict(self.clock)\n\n    @classmethod\n    def from_dict(cls, data: dict) -> \"VectorClock\":\n        return cls(data)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Service Class\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass AlbumCollaborationService:\n    \"\"\"Service for managing collaborative album design editing.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession):\n        self.db = db\n        self._lamport_counters: dict[str, int] = {}\n\n    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Session Management\n    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    async def create_session(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        album_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Create or join a collaborative album editing session.\n\n        Args:\n            workspace_id: Workspace UUID\n            album_id: Album UUID\n            user_id: User UUID\n\n        Returns:\n            Session data including session_id, collaborators, websocket info\n        \"\"\"\n        redis = await get_redis_client()\n\n        # Check for existing active session\n        existing_session = await self._get_active_session(album_id)\n\n        if existing_session:\n            session_id = UUID(existing_session[\"session_id\"])\n            color = await self._assign_collaborator_color(session_id, user_id)\n\n            await self._add_participant(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                user_id=user_id,\n                workspace_id=workspace_id,\n                color=color,\n            )\n\n            session_data = await self._get_session_data(session_id)\n            logger.info(f\"User {user_id} joined album session {session_id}\")\n        else:\n            session_id = await self._create_new_session(\n                workspace_id=workspace_id,\n                album_id=album_id,\n            )\n            color = await self._assign_collaborator_color(session_id, user_id)\n\n            await self._add_participant(\n                session_id=session_id,\n                user_id=user_id,\n                workspace_id=workspace_id,\n                color=color,\n            )\n\n            session_data = await self._get_session_data(session_id)\n            logger.info(f\"User {user_id} created album session {session_id}\")\n\n        # Cache session in Redis\n        await redis.setex(\n            f\"{ALBUM_SESSION_PREFIX}{session_id}\",\n            DEFAULT_SESSION_TTL,\n            json.dumps(session_data, default=str),\n        )\n\n        return {\n            \"session\": session_data,\n            \"your_color\": color,\n            \"websocket_url\": f\"/api/v1/albums/collaboration/ws/{session_id}\",\n            \"token\": await self._generate_session_token(session_id, user_id),\n        }\n\n    async def _get_active_session(self, album_id: UUID) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n        \"\"\"Get active session for an album.\"\"\"\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT session_id, workspace_id, album_id, status, version,\n                       crdt_state, created_at, last_activity\n                FROM album_collaboration_sessions\n                WHERE album_id = :album_id AND status = 'active'\n                ORDER BY created_at DESC\n                LIMIT 1\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"album_id\": str(album_id)},\n        )\n        row = result.fetchone()\n        if row:\n            return {\n                \"session_id\": str(row[0]),\n                \"workspace_id\": str(row[1]),\n                \"album_id\": str(row[2]),\n                \"status\": row[3],\n                \"version\": row[4],\n                \"crdt_state\": row[5] or {},\n                \"created_at\": row[6].isoformat() if row[6] else None,\n                \"last_activity\": row[7].isoformat() if row[7] else None,\n            }\n        return None\n\n    async def _create_new_session(\n        self,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        album_id: UUID,\n    ) -> UUID:\n        \"\"\"Create a new album collaboration session.\"\"\"\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                INSERT INTO album_collaboration_sessions\n                (workspace_id, album_id, status, version, crdt_state)\n                VALUES (:workspace_id, :album_id, 'active', 1, '{}')\n                RETURNING session_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\n                \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n                \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n            },\n        )\n        await self.db.commit()\n        row = result.fetchone()\n        return UUID(str(row[0]))\n\n    async def _add_participant(\n        self,\n        session_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        color: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Add a participant to a session.\"\"\"\n        # Check if already exists\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT participant_id FROM album_collaboration_participants\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id AND user_id = :user_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"session_id\": str(session_id), \"user_id\": str(user_id)},\n        )\n        existing = result.fetchone()\n\n        if existing:\n            await self.db.execute(\n                text(\"\"\"\n                    UPDATE album_collaboration_participants\n                    SET status = 'active', left_at = NULL, last_activity = NOW()\n                    WHERE session_id = :session_id AND user_id = :user_id\n                \"\"\"),\n                {\"session_id\": str(session_id), \"user_id\": str(user_id)},\n            )\n        else:\n            await self.db.execute(\n                text(\"\"\"\n                    INSERT INTO album_collaboration_participants\n                    (session_id, user_id, workspace_id, color, status)\n                    VALUES (:session_id, :user_id, :workspace_id, :color, 'active')\n                \"\"\"),\n                {\n                    \"session_id\": str(session_id),\n                    \"user_id\": str(user_id),\n                    \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n                    \"color\": color,\n                },\n            )\n        await self.db.commit()\n\n    async def _assign_collaborator_color(\n        self,\n        session_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Assign a unique color to a collaborator.\"\"\"\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT color FROM album_collaboration_participants\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id AND user_id != :user_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"session_id\": str(session_id), \"user_id\": str(user_id)},\n        )\n        used_colors = {row[0] for row in result.fetchall()}\n\n        for color in COLLABORATOR_COLORS:\n            if color not in used_colors:\n                return color\n\n        return f\"#{secrets.token_hex(3)}\"\n\n    async def _get_session_data(self, session_id: UUID) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Get full session data including collaborators.\"\"\"\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT session_id, workspace_id, album_id, status, version,\n                       crdt_state, created_at, last_activity\n                FROM album_collaboration_sessions\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"session_id\": str(session_id)},\n        )\n        session_row = result.fetchone()\n\n        if not session_row:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Session {session_id} not found\")\n\n        # Get collaborators\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT p.user_id, p.color, p.status, p.cursor_position,\n                       p.selected_elements, p.current_spread_id, p.joined_at, p.last_activity,\n                       u.email, u.first_name, u.last_name\n                FROM album_collaboration_participants p\n                JOIN users u ON p.user_id = u.user_id\n                WHERE p.session_id = :session_id AND p.status != 'disconnected'\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"session_id\": str(session_id)},\n        )\n        collaborators = []\n        for row in result.fetchall():\n            display_name = f\"{row[8] or ''} {row[9] or ''}\".strip() or row[10]\n            collaborators.append({\n                \"user_id\": str(row[0]),\n                \"display_name\": display_name,\n                \"color\": row[1],\n                \"status\": row[2],\n                \"cursor\": row[3],\n                \"selected_elements\": row[4] or [],\n                \"current_spread_id\": str(row[5]) if row[5] else None,\n                \"joined_at\": row[6].isoformat() if row[6] else None,\n                \"last_activity\": row[7].isoformat() if row[7] else None,\n            })\n\n        return {\n            \"session_id\": str(session_row[0]),\n            \"workspace_id\": str(session_row[1]),\n            \"album_id\": str(session_row[2]),\n            \"status\": session_row[3],\n            \"version\": session_row[4],\n            \"crdt_state\": session_row[5] or {},\n            \"created_at\": session_row[6].isoformat() if session_row[6] else None,\n            \"last_activity\": session_row[7].isoformat() if session_row[7] else None,\n            \"collaborators\": collaborators,\n        }\n\n    async def _generate_session_token(\n        self,\n        session_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n    ) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Generate a token for WebSocket authentication.\"\"\"\n        redis = await get_redis_client()\n        token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n        token_data = {\n            \"session_id\": str(session_id),\n            \"user_id\": str(user_id),\n        }\n        await redis.setex(\n            f\"album:token:{token}\",\n            DEFAULT_SESSION_TTL,\n            json.dumps(token_data),\n        )\n        return token\n\n    async def validate_session_token(self, token: str) -> dict[str, str] | None:\n        \"\"\"Validate a session token.\"\"\"\n        redis = await get_redis_client()\n        data = await redis.get(f\"album:token:{token}\")\n        if data:\n            return json.loads(data)\n        return None\n\n    async def leave_session(\n        self,\n        session_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Mark a user as leaving a session.\"\"\"\n        await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                UPDATE album_collaboration_participants\n                SET status = 'disconnected', left_at = NOW()\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id AND user_id = :user_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"session_id\": str(session_id), \"user_id\": str(user_id)},\n        )\n        await self.db.commit()\n\n        # Check if session is empty\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT COUNT(*) FROM album_collaboration_participants\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id AND status = 'active'\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"session_id\": str(session_id)},\n        )\n        count = result.fetchone()[0]\n\n        if count == 0:\n            await self.db.execute(\n                text(\"\"\"\n                    UPDATE album_collaboration_sessions\n                    SET status = 'closed', closed_at = NOW()\n                    WHERE session_id = :session_id\n                \"\"\"),\n                {\"session_id\": str(session_id)},\n            )\n            await self.db.commit()\n\n        # Broadcast leave event\n        await self._broadcast_presence_update(\n            session_id=session_id,\n            user_id=user_id,\n            event=\"left\",\n        )\n\n        logger.info(f\"User {user_id} left album session {session_id}\")\n\n    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Presence Management\n    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    async def update_presence(\n        self,\n        session_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n        cursor: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n        selected_elements: list[str] | None = None,\n        current_spread_id: UUID | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Update a user's presence in a session.\"\"\"\n        redis = await get_redis_client()\n\n        updates = [\"last_activity = NOW()\"]\n        params = {\n            \"session_id\": str(session_id),\n            \"user_id\": str(user_id),\n        }\n\n        if cursor is not None:\n            updates.append(\"cursor_position = :cursor\")\n            params[\"cursor\"] = json.dumps(cursor)\n\n        if selected_elements is not None:\n            updates.append(\"selected_elements = :selected\")\n            params[\"selected\"] = selected_elements\n\n        if current_spread_id is not None:\n            updates.append(\"current_spread_id = :spread_id\")\n            params[\"spread_id\"] = str(current_spread_id)\n\n        await self.db.execute(\n            text(f\"\"\"\n                UPDATE album_collaboration_participants\n                SET {\", \".join(updates)}\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id AND user_id = :user_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            params,\n        )\n        await self.db.commit()\n\n        # Cache cursor in Redis for fast access\n        if cursor:\n            await redis.setex(\n                f\"{ALBUM_CURSOR_PREFIX}{session_id}:{user_id}\",\n                DEFAULT_CURSOR_TTL,\n                json.dumps(cursor),\n            )\n\n        # Broadcast update\n        await self._broadcast_presence_update(\n            session_id=session_id,\n            user_id=user_id,\n            event=\"updated\",\n            cursor=cursor,\n            selected_elements=selected_elements,\n            current_spread_id=str(current_spread_id) if current_spread_id else None,\n        )\n\n    async def _broadcast_presence_update(\n        self,\n        session_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n        event: str,\n        cursor: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n        selected_elements: list[str] | None = None,\n        current_spread_id: str | None = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Broadcast presence update to all participants.\"\"\"\n        redis = await get_redis_client()\n\n        message = {\n            \"type\": \"album:presence\",\n            \"session_id\": str(session_id),\n            \"user_id\": str(user_id),\n            \"event\": event,\n            \"timestamp\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n        }\n        if cursor:\n            message[\"cursor\"] = cursor\n        if selected_elements:\n            message[\"selected_elements\"] = selected_elements\n        if current_spread_id:\n            message[\"current_spread_id\"] = current_spread_id\n\n        await redis.publish(\n            f\"{ALBUM_CHANNEL_PREFIX}{session_id}\",\n            json.dumps(message),\n        )\n\n    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Operation Management (CRDT)\n    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n    async def apply_operation(\n        self,\n        session_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        album_id: UUID,\n        operation_type: str,\n        target_type: str,\n        target_id: UUID,\n        payload: dict[str, Any],\n        vector_clock: dict[str, int],\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Apply a collaborative operation using CRDT semantics.\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session UUID\n            user_id: User performing the operation\n            workspace_id: Workspace UUID\n            album_id: Album UUID\n            operation_type: Type of operation\n            target_type: Type of target (album, spread, element, comment)\n            target_id: Target UUID\n            payload: Operation payload\n            vector_clock: Client's vector clock\n\n        Returns:\n            Operation result including new version and any conflicts\n        \"\"\"\n        # Get session version\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT version, crdt_state FROM album_collaboration_sessions\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id\n                FOR UPDATE\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"session_id\": str(session_id)},\n        )\n        row = result.fetchone()\n        current_version = row[0] if row else 1\n        crdt_state = row[1] or {} if row else {}\n\n        # Generate Lamport timestamp\n        session_key = str(session_id)\n        self._lamport_counters[session_key] = max(\n            self._lamport_counters.get(session_key, 0),\n            max(vector_clock.values()) if vector_clock else 0\n        ) + 1\n        lamport_timestamp = self._lamport_counters[session_key]\n\n        # Update vector clock\n        user_key = str(user_id)\n        vector_clock[user_key] = vector_clock.get(user_key, 0) + 1\n\n        # Check for concurrent operations\n        is_conflicted = await self._check_concurrent_operations(\n            session_id=session_id,\n            target_type=target_type,\n            target_id=target_id,\n            lamport_timestamp=lamport_timestamp,\n        )\n\n        conflict_resolution = None\n        if is_conflicted:\n            # Apply LWW resolution\n            conflict_resolution = {\n                \"strategy\": \"last_writer_wins\",\n                \"lamport_timestamp\": lamport_timestamp,\n                \"resolved_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n            }\n\n        # Record operation\n        new_version = current_version + 1\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                INSERT INTO album_operations\n                (session_id, album_id, workspace_id, user_id, operation_type,\n                 target_type, target_id, payload, vector_clock, lamport_timestamp,\n                 base_version, result_version, is_applied, is_conflicted, conflict_resolution)\n                VALUES (:session_id, :album_id, :workspace_id, :user_id, :operation_type,\n                        :target_type, :target_id, :payload, :vector_clock, :lamport_timestamp,\n                        :base_version, :result_version, TRUE, :is_conflicted, :conflict_resolution)\n                RETURNING operation_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\n                \"session_id\": str(session_id),\n                \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n                \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n                \"user_id\": str(user_id),\n                \"operation_type\": operation_type,\n                \"target_type\": target_type,\n                \"target_id\": str(target_id),\n                \"payload\": json.dumps(payload),\n                \"vector_clock\": json.dumps(vector_clock),\n                \"lamport_timestamp\": lamport_timestamp,\n                \"base_version\": current_version,\n                \"result_version\": new_version,\n                \"is_conflicted\": is_conflicted,\n                \"conflict_resolution\": json.dumps(conflict_resolution) if conflict_resolution else None,\n            },\n        )\n        op_row = result.fetchone()\n        operation_id = str(op_row[0])\n\n        # Update session version\n        await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                UPDATE album_collaboration_sessions\n                SET version = :version, last_activity = NOW()\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"version\": new_version, \"session_id\": str(session_id)},\n        )\n        await self.db.commit()\n\n        # Apply operation to actual data\n        await self._apply_operation_to_data(\n            album_id=album_id,\n            workspace_id=workspace_id,\n            user_id=user_id,\n            operation_type=operation_type,\n            target_type=target_type,\n            target_id=target_id,\n            payload=payload,\n        )\n\n        # Broadcast operation\n        await self._broadcast_operation(\n            session_id=session_id,\n            user_id=user_id,\n            operation_id=operation_id,\n            operation_type=operation_type,\n            target_type=target_type,\n            target_id=target_id,\n            payload=payload,\n            new_version=new_version,\n            vector_clock=vector_clock,\n            lamport_timestamp=lamport_timestamp,\n        )\n\n        return {\n            \"operation_id\": operation_id,\n            \"success\": True,\n            \"new_version\": new_version,\n            \"vector_clock\": vector_clock,\n            \"lamport_timestamp\": lamport_timestamp,\n            \"is_conflicted\": is_conflicted,\n            \"conflict_resolution\": conflict_resolution,\n        }\n\n    async def _check_concurrent_operations(\n        self,\n        session_id: UUID,\n        target_type: str,\n        target_id: UUID,\n        lamport_timestamp: int,\n    ) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if there are concurrent operations on the same target.\"\"\"\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT operation_id FROM album_operations\n                WHERE session_id = :session_id\n                  AND target_type = :target_type\n                  AND target_id = :target_id\n                  AND lamport_timestamp >= :timestamp - 1\n                  AND is_applied = TRUE\n                  AND created_at > NOW() - INTERVAL '5 seconds'\n                LIMIT 1\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\n                \"session_id\": str(session_id),\n                \"target_type\": target_type,\n                \"target_id\": str(target_id),\n                \"timestamp\": lamport_timestamp,\n            },\n        )\n        return result.fetchone() is not None\n\n    async def _apply_operation_to_data(\n        self,\n        album_id: UUID,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        user_id: UUID,\n        operation_type: str,\n        target_type: str,\n        target_id: UUID,\n        payload: dict[str, Any],\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Apply the operation to the actual database data.\"\"\"\n        if target_type == \"element\":\n            await self._apply_element_operation(\n                album_id, workspace_id, operation_type, target_id, payload\n            )\n        elif target_type == \"spread\":\n            await self._apply_spread_operation(\n                album_id, workspace_id, operation_type, target_id, payload\n            )\n        elif target_type == \"album\":\n            await self._apply_album_operation(\n                album_id, workspace_id, user_id, operation_type, payload\n            )\n        elif target_type == \"comment\":\n            await self._apply_comment_operation(\n                album_id, workspace_id, user_id, operation_type, target_id, payload\n            )\n\n    async def _apply_element_operation(\n        self,\n        album_id: UUID,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        operation_type: str,\n        element_id: UUID,\n        payload: dict[str, Any],\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Apply element-level operations.\"\"\"\n        if operation_type == AlbumOperationType.ELEMENT_CREATE.value:\n            await self.db.execute(\n                text(\"\"\"\n                    INSERT INTO album_spread_elements\n                    (element_id, spread_id, album_id, workspace_id, element_type,\n                     asset_id, position, content, style, effects, crop_config)\n                    VALUES (:element_id, :spread_id, :album_id, :workspace_id, :element_type,\n                            :asset_id, :position, :content, :style, :effects, :crop_config)\n                \"\"\"),\n                {\n                    \"element_id\": str(element_id),\n                    \"spread_id\": str(payload[\"spread_id\"]),\n                    \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n                    \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n                    \"element_type\": payload.get(\"element_type\", \"photo\"),\n                    \"asset_id\": str(payload[\"asset_id\"]) if payload.get(\"asset_id\") else None,\n                    \"position\": json.dumps(payload.get(\"position\", {})),\n                    \"content\": json.dumps(payload.get(\"content\", {})),\n                    \"style\": json.dumps(payload.get(\"style\", {})),\n                    \"effects\": json.dumps(payload.get(\"effects\", [])),\n                    \"crop_config\": json.dumps(payload.get(\"crop_config\", {})),\n                },\n            )\n        elif operation_type in [\n            AlbumOperationType.ELEMENT_UPDATE.value,\n            AlbumOperationType.ELEMENT_MOVE.value,\n            AlbumOperationType.ELEMENT_RESIZE.value,\n            AlbumOperationType.ELEMENT_ROTATE.value,\n            AlbumOperationType.ELEMENT_STYLE.value,\n        ]:\n            updates = []\n            params = {\n                \"element_id\": str(element_id),\n                \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n            }\n\n            if \"position\" in payload:\n                updates.append(\"position = :position\")\n                params[\"position\"] = json.dumps(payload[\"position\"])\n            if \"content\" in payload:\n                updates.append(\"content = :content\")\n                params[\"content\"] = json.dumps(payload[\"content\"])\n            if \"style\" in payload:\n                updates.append(\"style = :style\")\n                params[\"style\"] = json.dumps(payload[\"style\"])\n            if \"effects\" in payload:\n                updates.append(\"effects = :effects\")\n                params[\"effects\"] = json.dumps(payload[\"effects\"])\n            if \"crop_config\" in payload:\n                updates.append(\"crop_config = :crop_config\")\n                params[\"crop_config\"] = json.dumps(payload[\"crop_config\"])\n\n            if updates:\n                updates.append(\"updated_at = NOW()\")\n                await self.db.execute(\n                    text(f\"\"\"\n                        UPDATE album_spread_elements\n                        SET {\", \".join(updates)}\n                        WHERE element_id = :element_id AND workspace_id = :workspace_id\n                    \"\"\"),\n                    params,\n                )\n        elif operation_type == AlbumOperationType.ELEMENT_DELETE.value:\n            await self.db.execute(\n                text(\"\"\"\n                    DELETE FROM album_spread_elements\n                    WHERE element_id = :element_id AND workspace_id = :workspace_id\n                \"\"\"),\n                {\n                    \"element_id\": str(element_id),\n                    \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n                },\n            )\n\n        await self.db.commit()\n\n    async def _apply_spread_operation(\n        self,\n        album_id: UUID,\n        workspace_id: UUID,\n        operation_type: str,\n        spread_id: UUID,\n        payload: dict[str, Any],\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Apply spread-level operations.\"\"\"\n        if operation_type == AlbumOperationType.SPREAD_CREATE.value:\n            # Get max spread number\n            result = await self.db.execute(\n                text(\"\"\"\n                    SELECT CreateElementRequest(BaseModel):\n    spread_id: UUID\n    element_type: str = Field(..., pattern=\"^(photo|text|shape|frame|decoration|qr_code|page_number)$\")\n    asset_id: UUID | None = None\n    position: ElementPosition = Field(default_factory=ElementPosition)\n    content: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n    style: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n    effects: list[dict[str, Any]] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    crop_config: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=lambda: {\"x\": 0, \"y\": 0, \"width\": 100, \"height\": 100})\n\n\nclass UpdateElementRequest(BaseModel):\n    position: ElementPosition | None = None\n    content: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n    style: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n    effects: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None\n    crop_config: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n\n\nclass CreateCommentRequest(BaseModel):\n    spread_id: UUID | None = None\n    element_id: UUID | None = None\n    parent_comment_id: UUID | None = None\n    content: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=5000)\n    mentions: list[UUID] = Field(default_factory=list)\n    position: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n\n\nclass ResolveCommentRequest(BaseModel):\n    comment_ids: list[UUID]\n\n\nclass CollaborativeOperationRequest(BaseModel):\n    operation_type: str\n    target_type: str = Field(..., pattern=\"^(album|spread|element|comment)$\")\n    target_id: UUID\n    payload: dict[str, Any]\n    vector_clock: dict[str, int] = Field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\nclass RollbackRequest(BaseModel):\n    version_number: int = Field(..., ge=1)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Album CRUD Endpoints\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n@router.post(\"\", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)\nasync def create_album(\n    request: CreateAlbumRequest,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Create a new album.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    album_id = uuid4()\n\n    # If template is provided, get template settings\n    template_settings = {}\n    if request.template_id:\n        result = await db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT page_size, default_spread_layout, color_palette, typography\n                FROM album_templates\n                WHERE template_id = :template_id AND (workspace_id = :workspace_id OR is_system = TRUE)\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"template_id\": str(request.template_id), \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id)},\n        )\n        row = result.fetchone()\n        if row:\n            template_settings = {\n                \"page_size\": row[0],\n                \"default_spread_layout\": row[1],\n                \"color_palette\": row[2],\n                \"typography\": row[3],\n            }\n\n    page_size = template_settings.get(\"page_size\") or request.page_size.model_dump()\n\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO albums\n            (album_id, workspace_id, gallery_id, template_id, title, description,\n             album_type, status, page_size, design_config, created_by)\n            VALUES (:album_id, :workspace_id, :gallery_id, :template_id, :title, :description,\n                    :album_type, 'draft', :page_size, :design_config, :created_by)\n            RETURNING album_id, created_at\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\n            \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n            \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n            \"gallery_id\": str(request.gallery_id) if request.gallery_id else None,\n            \"template_id\": str(request.template_id) if request.template_id else None,\n            \"title\": request.title,\n            \"description\": request.description,\n            \"album_type\": request.album_type,\n            \"page_size\": json.dumps(page_size),\n            \"design_config\": json.dumps(template_settings.get(\"typography\", {})),\n            \"created_by\": str(current_user.user_id),\n        },\n    )\n    await db.commit()\n\n    # Create initial spreads (cover + first spread)\n    for idx, spread_type in enumerate([\"cover_front\", \"double\"]):\n        await db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                INSERT INTO album_spreads\n                (album_id, workspace_id, spread_number, spread_type, layout_config, background_config)\n                VALUES (:album_id, :workspace_id, :spread_number, :spread_type, :layout_config, :background_config)\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\n                \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n                \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n                \"spread_number\": idx + 1,\n                \"spread_type\": spread_type,\n                \"layout_config\": json.dumps(template_settings.get(\"default_spread_layout\", {})),\n                \"background_config\": json.dumps({\"type\": \"color\", \"value\": \"#ffffff\"}),\n            },\n        )\n    await db.commit()\n\n    # Create initial version\n    service = get_album_collaboration_service(db)\n    await service.create_version_snapshot(\n        album_id=album_id,\n        workspace_id=workspace_id,\n        user_id=current_user.user_id,\n        change_type=\"create\",\n        change_summary=\"Album created\",\n    )\n\n    return {\n        \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n        \"title\": request.title,\n        \"album_type\": request.album_type,\n        \"status\": \"draft\",\n        \"page_size\": page_size,\n    }\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\nasync def list_albums(\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n    status: str | None = Query(None, pattern=\"^(draft|in_review|approved|ordered|archived)$\"),\n    album_type: str | None = Query(None),\n    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"List albums for the workspace.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    conditions = [\"workspace_id = :workspace_id\"]\n    params: dict[str, Any] = {\"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id), \"limit\": limit, \"offset\": offset}\n\n    if status:\n        conditions.append(\"status = :status\")\n        params[\"status\"] = status\n\n    if album_type:\n        conditions.append(\"album_type = :album_type\")\n        params[\"album_type\"] = album_type\n\n    where_clause = \" AND \".join(conditions)\n\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(f\"\"\"\n            SELECT album_id, title, description, album_type, status,\n                   page_count, created_at, updated_at\n            FROM albums\n            WHERE {where_clause}\n            ORDER BY updated_at DESC\n            LIMIT :limit OFFSET :offset\n        \"\"\"),\n        params,\n    )\n\n    albums = [\n        {\n            \"album_id\": str(row[0]),\n            \"title\": row[1],\n            \"description\": row[2],\n            \"album_type\": row[3],\n            \"status\": row[4],\n            \"page_count\": row[5],\n            \"created_at\": row[6].isoformat() if row[6] else None,\n            \"updated_at\": row[7].isoformat() if row[7] else None,\n        }\n        for row in result.fetchall()\n    ]\n\n    # Get total count\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(f\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM albums WHERE {where_clause}\"),\n        params,\n    )\n    total = result.fetchone()[0]\n\n    return {\n        \"albums\": albums,\n        \"total\": total,\n        \"limit\": limit,\n        \"offset\": offset,\n    }\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{album_id}\")\nasync def get_album(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Get album with all spreads and elements.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    # Get album\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            SELECT a.album_id, a.title, a.description, a.album_type, a.status,\n                   a.page_size, a.page_count, a.cover_config, a.spine_config,\n                   a.design_config, a.current_version, a.created_at, a.updated_at,\n                   t.name as template_name\n            FROM albums a\n            LEFT JOIN album_templates t ON a.template_id = t.template_id\n            WHERE a.album_id = :album_id AND a.workspace_id = :workspace_id\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\"album_id\": str(album_id), \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id)},\n    )\n    album_row = result.fetchone()\n\n    if not album_row:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Album not found\")\n\n    # Get spreads\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            SELECT spread_id, spread_number, spread_type, layout_template_id,\n                   layout_config, background_config, is_locked\n            FROM album_spreads\n            WHERE album_id = :album_id\n            ORDER BY spread_number\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\"album_id\": str(album_id)},\n    )\n    spreads = []\n    spread_ids = []\n    for row in result.fetchall():\n        spread_ids.append(str(row[0]))\n        spreads.append({\n            \"spread_id\": str(row[0]),\n            \"spread_number\": row[1],\n            \"spread_type\": row[2],\n            \"layout_template_id\": row[3],\n            \"layout_config\": row[4],\n            \"background_config\": row[5],\n            \"is_locked\": row[6],\n            \"elements\": [],\n        })\n\n    # Get elements for all spreads\n    if spread_ids:\n        result = await db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                SELECT element_id, spread_id, element_type, asset_id,\n                       position, content, style, effects, crop_config,\n                       is_locked, is_visible, layer_name, group_id\n                FROM album_spread_elements\n                WHERE album_id = :album_id\n                ORDER BY (position->>'z_index')::int\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"album_id\": str(album_id)},\n        )\n\n        elements_by_spread: dict[str, list] = {sid: [] for sid in spread_ids}\n        for row in result.fetchall():\n            spread_id = str(row[1])\n            if spread_id in elements_by_spread:\n                elements_by_spread[spread_id].append({\n                    \"element_id\": str(row[0]),\n                    \"element_type\": row[2],\n                    \"asset_id\": str(row[3]) if row[3] else None,\n                    \"position\": row[4],\n                    \"content\": row[5],\n                    \"style\": row[6],\n                    \"effects\": row[7],\n                    \"crop_config\": row[8],\n                    \"is_locked\": row[9],\n                    \"is_visible\": row[10],\n                    \"layer_name\": row[11],\n                    \"group_id\": str(row[12]) if row[12] else None,\n                })\n\n        for spread in spreads:\n            spread[\"elements\"] = elements_by_spread.get(spread[\"spread_id\"], [])\n\n    return {\n        \"album_id\": str(album_row[0]),\n        \"title\": album_row[1],\n        \"description\": album_row[2],\n        \"album_type\": album_row[3],\n        \"status\": album_row[4],\n        \"page_size\": album_row[5],\n        \"page_count\": album_row[6],\n        \"cover_config\": album_row[7],\n        \"spine_config\": album_row[8],\n        \"design_config\": album_row[9],\n        \"current_version\": album_row[10],\n        \"template_name\": album_row[13],\n        \"spreads\": spreads,\n        \"created_at\": album_row[11].isoformat() if album_row[11] else None,\n        \"updated_at\": album_row[12].isoformat() if album_row[12] else None,\n    }\n\n\n@router.patch(\"/{album_id}\")\nasync def update_album(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    request: UpdateAlbumRequest,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Update album details.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    updates = [\"last_edited_by = :user_id\", \"last_edited_at = NOW()\", \"updated_at = NOW()\"]\n    params: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n        \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n        \"user_id\": str(current_user.user_id),\n    }\n\n    if request.title is not None:\n        updates.append(\"title = :title\")\n        params[\"title\"] = request.title\n    if request.description is not None:\n        updates.append(\"description = :description\")\n        params[\"description\"] = request.description\n    if request.status is not None:\n        updates.append(\"status = :status\")\n        params[\"status\"] = request.status\n    if request.design_config is not None:\n        updates.append(\"design_config = :design_config\")\n        params[\"design_config\"] = json.dumps(request.design_config)\n    if request.cover_config is not None:\n        updates.append(\"cover_config = :cover_config\")\n        params[\"cover_config\"] = json.dumps(request.cover_config)\n\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(f\"\"\"\n            UPDATE albums\n            SET {\", \".join(updates)}\n            WHERE album_id = :album_id AND workspace_id = :workspace_id\n            RETURNING album_id, title, status, updated_at\n        \"\"\"),\n        params,\n    )\n    row = result.fetchone()\n    await db.commit()\n\n    if not row:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Album not found\")\n\n    return {\n        \"album_id\": str(row[0]),\n        \"title\": row[1],\n        \"status\": row[2],\n        \"updated_at\": row[3].isoformat() if row[3] else None,\n    }\n\n\n@router.delete(\"/{album_id}\", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)\nasync def delete_album(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Delete an album.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            DELETE FROM albums\n            WHERE album_id = :album_id AND workspace_id = :workspace_id\n            RETURNING album_id\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\"album_id\": str(album_id), \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id)},\n    )\n    row = result.fetchone()\n    await db.commit()\n\n    if not row:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Album not found\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Spread Endpoints\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n@router.post(\"/{album_id}/spreads\", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)\nasync def create_spread(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    request: CreateSpreadRequest,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Create a new spread in the album.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    # Get max spread number\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            SELECT COALESCE(MAX(spread_number), 0) + 1\n            FROM album_spreads\n            WHERE album_id = :album_id\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\"album_id\": str(album_id)},\n    )\n    spread_number = result.fetchone()[0]\n\n    if request.position is not None and request.position < spread_number:\n        # Shift existing spreads\n        await db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                UPDATE album_spreads\n                SET spread_number = spread_number + 1\n                WHERE album_id = :album_id AND spread_number >= :position\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\"album_id\": str(album_id), \"position\": request.position},\n        )\n        spread_number = request.position\n\n    spread_id = uuid4()\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO album_spreads\n            (spread_id, album_id, workspace_id, spread_number, spread_type,\n             layout_template_id, layout_config, background_config)\n            VALUES (:spread_id, :album_id, :workspace_id, :spread_number, :spread_type,\n                    :layout_template_id, :layout_config, :background_config)\n            RETURNING spread_id, spread_number\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\n            \"spread_id\": str(spread_id),\n            \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n            \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n            \"spread_number\": spread_number,\n            \"spread_type\": request.spread_type,\n            \"layout_template_id\": request.layout_template_id,\n            \"layout_config\": json.dumps(request.layout_config),\n            \"background_config\": json.dumps(request.background_config),\n        },\n    )\n    row = result.fetchone()\n    await db.commit()\n\n    return {\n        \"spread_id\": str(row[0]),\n        \"spread_number\": row[1],\n        \"spread_type\": request.spread_type,\n    }\n\n\n@router.patch(\"/{album_id}/spreads/{spread_id}\")\nasync def update_spread(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    spread_id: UUID,\n    request: UpdateSpreadRequest,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Update a spread.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    updates = [\"updated_at = NOW()\"]\n    params: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"spread_id\": str(spread_id),\n        \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n        \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n    }\n\n    if request.layout_template_id is not None:\n        updates.append(\"layout_template_id = :layout_template_id\")\n        params[\"layout_template_id\"] = request.layout_template_id\n    if request.layout_config is not None:\n        updates.append(\"layout_config = :layout_config\")\n        params[\"layout_config\"] = json.dumps(request.layout_config)\n    if request.background_config is not None:\n        updates.append(\"background_config = :background_config\")\n        params[\"background_config\"] = json.dumps(request.background_config)\n\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(f\"\"\"\n            UPDATE album_spreads\n            SET {\", \".join(updates)}\n            WHERE spread_id = :spread_id AND album_id = :album_id AND workspace_id = :workspace_id\n            RETURNING spread_id, spread_number, spread_type\n        \"\"\"),\n        params,\n    )\n    row = result.fetchone()\n    await db.commit()\n\n    if not row:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Spread not found\")\n\n    return {\n        \"spread_id\": str(row[0]),\n        \"spread_number\": row[1],\n        \"spread_type\": row[2],\n    }\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{album_id}/spreads/reorder\")\nasync def reorder_spreads(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    request: ReorderSpreadsRequest,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Reorder spreads in the album.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    for idx, spread_id in enumerate(request.spread_ids):\n        await db.execute(\n            text(\"\"\"\n                UPDATE album_spreads\n                SET spread_number = :number, updated_at = NOW()\n                WHERE spread_id = :spread_id AND album_id = :album_id AND workspace_id = :workspace_id\n            \"\"\"),\n            {\n                \"spread_id\": str(spread_id),\n                \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n                \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n                \"number\": idx + 1,\n            },\n        )\n    await db.commit()\n\n    return {\"success\": True, \"spread_count\": len(request.spread_ids)}\n\n\n@router.delete(\"/{album_id}/spreads/{spread_id}\", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)\nasync def delete_spread(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    spread_id: UUID,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Delete a spread.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    # Get spread number for reordering\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            SELECT spread_number FROM album_spreads\n            WHERE spread_id = :spread_id AND album_id = :album_id AND workspace_id = :workspace_id\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\"spread_id\": str(spread_id), \"album_id\": str(album_id), \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id)},\n    )\n    row = result.fetchone()\n\n    if not row:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Spread not found\")\n\n    spread_number = row[0]\n\n    # Delete spread\n    await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            DELETE FROM album_spreads\n            WHERE spread_id = :spread_id AND workspace_id = :workspace_id\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\"spread_id\": str(spread_id), \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id)},\n    )\n\n    # Reorder remaining spreads\n    await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            UPDATE album_spreads\n            SET spread_number = spread_number - 1\n            WHERE album_id = :album_id AND spread_number > :number\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\"album_id\": str(album_id), \"number\": spread_number},\n    )\n    await db.commit()\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Element Endpoints\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n@router.post(\"/{album_id}/elements\", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)\nasync def create_element(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    request: CreateElementRequest,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Create a new element on a spread.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    element_id = uuid4()\n    result = await db.execute(\n        text(\"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO album_spread_elements\n            (element_id, spread_id, album_id, workspace_id, element_type,\n             asset_id, position, content, style, effects, crop_config)\n            VALUES (:element_id, :spread_id, :album_id, :workspace_id, :element_type,\n                    :asset_id, :position, :content, :style, :effects, :crop_config)\n            RETURNING element_id\n        \"\"\"),\n        {\n            \"element_id\": str(element_id),\n            \"spread_id\": str(request.spread_id),\n            \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n            \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n            \"element_type\": request.element_type,\n            \"asset_id\": str(request.asset_id) if request.asset_id else None,\n            \"position\": json.dumps(request.position.model_dump()),\n            \"content\": json.dumps(request.content),\n            \"style\": json.dumps(request.style),\n            \"effects\": json.dumps(request.effects),\n            \"crop_config\": json.dumps(request.crop_config),\n        },\n    )\n    await db.commit()\n\n    return {\n        \"element_id\": str(element_id),\n        \"spread_id\": str(request.spread_id),\n        \"element_type\": request.element_type,\n    }\n\n\n@router.patch(\"/{album_id}/elements/{element_id}\")\nasync def update_element(\n    album_id: UUID,\n    element_id: UUID,\n    request: UpdateElementRequest,\n    db: Annotated[AsyncSession, Depends(get_db)],\n    current_user: Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)],\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Update an element.\"\"\"\n    workspace_id = current_user.active_workspace_id\n    if not workspace_id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"No active workspace\")\n\n    updates = [\"updated_at = NOW()\"]\n    params: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"element_id\": str(element_id),\n        \"album_id\": str(album_id),\n        \"workspace_id\": str(workspace_id),\n    }\n\n    if request.position is not None:\n        updates.append(\"position = :position\")\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nclass AlbumConnectionManager:\n    \"\"\"Manages WebSocket connections for album collaboration.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.active_connections: dict[str, dict[str, WebSocket]] = {}\n\n    async def connect(self, session_id: str, user_id: str, websocket: WebSocket) -> None:\n        await websocket.accept()\n        if session_id not in self.active_connections:\n            self.active_connections[session_id] = {}\n        self.active_connections[session_id][user_id] = websocket\n\n    def disconnect(self, session_id: str, user_id: str) -> None:\n        if session_id in self.active_connections:\n            self.active_connections[session_id].pop(user_id, None)\n            if not self.active_connections[session_id]:\n                del self.active_connections[session_id]\n\n    async def broadcast_to_session(self, session_id: str, message: dict, exclude_user: str | None = None) -> None:\n        if session_id in self.active_connections:\n            for user_id, websocket in self.active_connections[session_id].items():\n                if user_id != exclude_user:\n                    try:\n                        await websocket.send_json(message)\n                    except Exception:\n                        pass\n\n\nmanager = AlbumConnectionManager()\n\n\n@router.websocket(\"/collaboration/ws/{session_id}\")\nasync def album_collaboration_websocket(\n    websocket: WebSocket,\n    session_id: UUID,\n    token: str = Query(...),\n):\n    \"\"\"WebSocket endpoint for real-time album collaboration.\"\"\"\n    from app.db.session import async_session_maker\n\n    # Validate token\n    redis = await get_redis_client()\n    token_data = await redis.get(f\"album:token:{token}\")\n\n    if not token_data:\n        await websocket.close(code=4001, reason=\"Invalid token\")\n        return\n\n    token_info = json.loads(token_data)\n    if token_info[\"session_id\"] != str(session_id):\n        await websocket.close(code=4002, reason=\"Token session mismatch\")\n        return\n\n    user_id = token_info[\"user_id\"]\n\n    # Connect\n    await manager.connect(str(session_id), user_id, websocket)\n\n    # Subscribe to Redis channel\n    pubsub = redis.pubsub()\n    await pubsub.subscribe(f\"album:channel:{session_id}\")\n\n    try:\n        # Send initial state\n        async with async_session_maker() as db:\n            service = get_album_collaboration_service(db)\n            session_data = await service._get_session_data(session_id)\n            await websocket.send_json({\n                \"type\": \"album:session_state\",\n                \"session\": session_data,\n            })\n\n        # Start listening for Redis messages in background\n        import asyncio\n\n        async def redis_listener():\n            async for message in pubsub.listen():\n                if message[\"type\"] == \"message\":\n                    data = json.loads(message[\"data\"])\n                    # Don't send to the user who originated the message\n                    if data.get(\"user_id\") != user_id:\n                        await websocket.send_json(data)\n\n        redis_task = asyncio.create_task(redis_listener())\n\n        # Handle incoming messages\n        while True:\n            data = await websocket.receive_json()\n            message_type = data.get(\"type\")\n\n            async with async_session_maker() as db:\n                service = get_album_collaboration_service(db)\n\n                if message_type == \"album:presence\":\n                    await service.update_presence(\n                        session_id=session_id,\n                        user_id=UUID(user_id),\n                        cursor=data.get(\"cursor\"),\n                        selected_elements=data.get(\"selected_elements\"),\n                        current_spread_id=UUID(data[\"current_spread_id\"]) if data.get(\"current_spread_id\") else None,\n                    )\n                elif message_type == \"album:operation\":\n                    # Operations go through REST API for proper CRDT handling\n                    pass\n                elif message_type == \"album:ping\":\n                    await websocket.send_json({\"type\": \"album:pong\"})\n\n    except WebSocketDisconnect:\n        logger.info(f\"User {user_id} disconnected from album session {session_id}\")\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.error(f\"WebSocket error: {e}\")\n    finally:\n        redis_task.cancel()\n        await pubsub.unsubscribe(f\"album:channel:{session_id}\")\n        manager.disconnect(str(session_id), user_id)\n\n        # Mark user as disconnected\n        async with async_session_maker() as db:\n            service = get_album_collaboration_service(db)\n            await service.leave_session(session_id, UUID(user_id))\n"
}

---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Enums\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Album status\n Album type/category\n */\nexport enum AlbumType {\n  WEDDING = 'wedding',\n  PORTRAIT = 'portrait',\n  EVENT = 'event',\n  FAMILY = 'family',\n  NEWBORN = 'newborn',\n  CORPORATE = 'corporate',\n  CUSTOM = 'custom',\n}\n\n/**\n * Spread type within an album\n */\nexport enum SpreadType {\n  COVER = 'cover',\n  FULL_SPREAD = 'full_spread',\n  SINGLE_PAGE = 'single_page',\n  BACK_COVER = EMBELLISHMENT = 'embellishment',\n}\n\n/**\n * Album collaboration operation types\n */\nexport enum AlbumOperationType {\n  // Album operations\n  ALBUM_UPDATE = 'album:update',\n  ALBUM_SETTINGS = 'album:settings',\n\n  // Spread operations\n  SPREAD_ADD = 'spread:add',\n  SPREAD_REMOVE = 'spread:remove',\n  SPREAD_REORDER = 'spread:reorder',\n  SPREAD_UPDATE = 'spread:update',\n  SPREAD_LAYOUT = 'spread:layout',\n\n  // Element operations\n  ELEMENT_ADD = 'element:add',\n  ELEMENT_REMOVE = 'element:remove',\n  ELEMENT_UPDATE = 'element:update',\n  ELEMENT_MOVE = 'element:move',\n  ELEMENT_RESIZE = 'element:resize',\n  ELEMENT_STYLE = 'element:style',\n\n  // Caption/text operations\n  CAPTION_UPDATE = 'caption:update',\n\n  // Cursor/selection (for presence)\n  CURSOR_MOVE = 'cursor:move',\n  SELECTION_CHANGE = 'selection:change',\n}\n\n/**\n * Comment status\n */\nexport enum CommentStatus {\n  OPEN = 'open',\n  RESOLVED = 'resolved',\n  DELETED = 'deleted',\n}\n\n/**\n unknown;\n  resolution_strategy: 'last_write_wins' | 'merge' | 'manual';\n}\n\n/**\n * Request to create a version snapshot\n */\nexport interface CreateVersionRequest {\n  name?: string;\n  description?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Request to rollback to a version\n */\nexport interface RollbackVersionRequest {\n  version_id: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Request to add a comment\n */\nexport CommentPosition;\n}\n\n/**\n * Request to resolve a comment\n */\nexport interface ResolveCommentRequest {\n  comment_id: string;\n}\n\n// AlbumCollaborationMessage {\n  type: AlbumCollaborationMessageType.PRESENCE_UPDATE;\n  collaborator: AlbumCollaboratorPresence;\n  event: 'joined' | 'left' | 'updated';\n}\n\n/**\n * Cursor update Comment added message\n */\nexport interface AlbumCommentAddedMessage extends AlbumCollaborationMessage {\n  type: AlbumCollaborationMessageType.COMMENT_ADDED;\n  comment: AlbumComment;\n}\n\n/**\n * Comment mention notification\n */\nexport interface AlbumCommentMentionMessage extends AlbumCollaborationMessage {\n  type: AlbumCollaborationMessageType.COMMENT_MENTION;\n  comment_id: string;\n  comment_preview: string;\n  mentioned_by: string;\n  mentioned_by_name: string;\n  spread_id?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Version created message\n */\nexport interface AlbumVersionCreatedMessage extends AlbumCollaborationMessage {\n  type: AlbumCollaborationMessageType.VERSION_CREATED;\n  version: AlbumVersion;\n}\n\n/**\n * Version restored message\n */\nexport interface AlbumVersionRestoredMessage extends AlbumCollaborationMessage {\n  type: AlbumCollaborationMessageType.VERSION_RESTORED;\n  version_number: number;\n  restored_by: string;\n  restored_by_name: string;\n  new_state: ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Query parameters for listing albums\n */\nexport interface ListAlbumsQuery {\n  gallery_id?: string;\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface AlbumEditorProps {\n  /** Album data */\n  album: Album;\n  /** Spreads in the album */\n  spreads: AlbumSpread[];\n  /** Currently selected spread index */\n  selectedSpreadIndex: number;\n  /** Selected element IDs */\n  selectedElementIds: string[];\n  /** Whether in collaboration mode */\n  isCollaborative: boolean;\n  /** Connection status for collaboration */\n  connectionStatus: 'disconnected' | 'connecting' | myColor: string;\n  /** Callback when spread is selected */\n  onSpreadSelect: (index: number) => void;\n  /** Callback when element(s) are selected */\n  onElementSelect: (elementIds: string[]) => void;\n  /** Callback when element is updated */\n  onElementUpdate: (elementId: string, updates: Partial<AlbumSpreadElement>) => void;\n  /** Callback when element is added */\n  onElementAdd: onStartCollaboration,\n  onStopCollaboration,\n  onUndo,\n  onRedo,\n  canUndo,\n  canRedo,\n  className = '',\n}) => {\n  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1);\n  const [showProperties, setShowProperties] = useState(true);\n  const [tool, setTool] = useState<'select' | 'pan' | 'text' | 'shape'>('select');\n  const editorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  const currentSpread = => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      // Check if we're in an input field\n      if (\n        e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement ||\n        e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement\n      ) {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Undo: Ctrl/Cmd + Z\n      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'z' && !e.shiftKey) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        if (canUndo) onUndo();\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Redo: Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + Z or Ctrl/Cmd + Y\n      if (\n        ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.shiftKey && e.key === 'z') ||\n        ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'y')\n      ) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        if (canRedo) onRedo();\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Delete selected elements\n      if ((e.key === deselect\n      if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n        onElementSelect([]);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Select all: Ctrl/Cmd + A\n      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && e.key === 'a') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        if (currentSpread) {\n          onElementSelect(currentSpread.elements.map((el) => el.id));\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  }, [canUndo, canRedo, onUndo, onRedo, selectedElementIds, onElementDelete, onElementSelect, currentSpread]);\n\n  // Handle cursor movement for collaboration\n  const handleMouseMove = useCallback(\n    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n      if (!isCollaborative || !onCursorMove || !currentSpread) return;\n\n      const rect = editorRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();\n      if (!rect) return;\n\n      const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) / zoom;\n      const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) / zoom;\n\n      onCursorMove({\n        spread_id: currentSpread.id,\n        x,\n        y,\n      });\n    },\n    [isCollaborative, onCursorMove, currentSpread, zoom]\n  );\n\n  // Get other collaborators on current spread\n  const collaboratorsOnSpread = collaborators.filter(\n    (c) =>\n      c.user_id !== currentUserId &&\n      c.current_spread_id === currentSpread?.id\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <div className={`flex flex-col h-full bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-950 ${className}`}>\n      {/* Toolbar */}\n      <AlbumToolbar\n        album={album}\n        tool={tool}\n        onToolChange={setTool}\n        zoom={zoom}\n        onZoomChange={setZoom}\n        onUndo={onUndo}\n        onRedo={onRedo}\n        canUndo={canUndo}\n        canRedo={canRedo}\n        onAddSpread={() => onSpreadAdd(selectedSpreadIndex + 1)}\n        showProperties={showProperties}\n        onToggleProperties={() => setShowProperties(!showProperties)}\n      />\n\n      {/* Collaboration Bar */}\n      {isCollaborative && (\n        <AlbumCollaborationBar\n          connectionStatus={connectionStatus}\n          collaborators={collaborators}\n          currentUserId={currentUserId}\n          myColor={myColor}\n          onStopCollaboration={onStopCollaboration}\n        />\n      )}\n\n      {/* Main Editor Area */}\n      <div className=\"flex flex-1 overflow-hidden\">\n        {/* Spread Thumbnail Strip (Left) */}\n        <SpreadThumbnailStrip\n          spreads={spreads}\n          selectedIndex={selectedSpreadIndex}\n          onSelect={onSpreadSelect}\n          onReorder={onSpreadReorder}\n          onAdd={onSpreadAdd}\n          onDelete={onSpreadDelete}\n          collaborators={collaborators}\n          currentUserId={currentUserId}\n          className=\"w-48 flex-shrink-0 border-r border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800\"\n        />\n\n        {/* Canvas Area */}\n        <div\n          ref={editorRef}\n          className=\"flex-1 overflow-auto relative\"\n          onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}\n        >\n          {currentSpread ? (\n            <SpreadCanvas\n              spread={currentSpread}\n              albumWidth={album.width}\n              albumHeight={album.height}\n              zoom={zoom}\n              tool={tool}\n              selectedElementIds={selectedElementIds}\n              onElementSelect={onElementSelect}\n              onElementUpdate={onElementUpdate}\n              onElementAdd={onElementAdd}\n              collaborators={collaboratorsOnSpread}\n              myColor={myColor}\n            />\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center h-full\">\n              <div className=\"text-center\">\n                <div className=\"text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-600 mb-4\">\n                  <svg\n                    className=\"w-16 h-16 mx-auto\"\n                    fill=\"none\"\n                    viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\"\n                    stroke=\"currentColor\"\n                  >\n                    <path\n                      strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                      strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                      strokeWidth={1.5}\n                      d=\"M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z\"\n                    />\n                  </svg>\n                </div>\n                <p className=\"text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mb-4\">\n                  No spreads in this album yet\n                </p>\n                <button\n                  onClick={() => onSpreadAdd()}\n                  className=\"px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-colors\"\n                >\n                  Add First Spread\n                </button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Properties Panel (Right) */}\n        {showProperties && (\n          <ElementPropertiesPanel\n            elements={selectedElements}\n            spread={currentSpread}\n            onElementUpdate={onElementUpdate}\n            onSpreadUpdate={onSpreadUpdate}\n            className=\"w-72 flex-shrink-0 border-l border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800\"\n          />\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Start Collaboration Button (when not collaborative) */}\n      {!isCollaborative && (\n        <button\n          onClick={onStartCollaboration}\n          className=\"absolute bottom-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-full shadow-lg hover:bg-blue-700 transition-colors\"\n        >\n          <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path\n              strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n              strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n              strokeWidth={2}\n              d=\"M17 20h5v-2a3 3 0 00-5.356-1.857M17 20H7m10 0v-2c0-.656-.126-1.283-.356-1.857M7 20H2v-2a3 3 0 015.356-1.857M7 20v-2c0-.656.126-1.283.356-1.857m0 0a5.002 5.002 0 019.288 0M15 7a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z\"\n            />\n          </svg>\n          Collaborate\n        </button>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default AlbumEditor;\n"
