albumHeight;\n\n  // Get mouse position relative to canvas\n  const getCanvasPosition = useCallback(\n    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n      const rect = canvasRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();\n      if (!rect) return { x: 0, y: 0 };\n      return {\n        x: (e.clientX - rect.left) / zoom,\n        y: (e.clientY - rect.top) / zoom,\n      };\n    },\n    [zoom]\n  );\n\n  // Handle mouse down on canvas\n  const handleCanvasMouseDown = useCallback(\n    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n      if (e.target !== canvasRef.current) return;\n\n      const pos = getCanvasPosition(e);\n\n      if (tool === 'select') {\n        // Start selection box\n        setSelectionBox({\n          isSelecting: true,\n          startX: pos.x,\n          startY: pos.y,\n          currentX: pos.x,\n          currentY: pos.y,\n        });\n\n        // Deselect if not holding shift\n        if (!e.shiftKey) {\n          onElementSelect([]);\n        }\n      } else if (tool === 'text') {\n        // Add text element\n        onElementAdd({\n          spread_id: spread.id,\n          element_type: 'text' as ElementType,\n          x: pos.x,\n          y: pos.y,\n          width: 200,\n          height: 50,\n          rotation: 0,\n          z_index: spread.elements.length + 1,\n          opacity: 1,\n          content: {\n            text: 'Double-click to edit',\n          },\n          style: {\n            font_family: 'Inter',\n            font_size: 16,\n            text_color: '#000000',\n            text_align: 'left',\n          },\n          locked: false,\n          visible: true,\n        });\n      }\n    },\n    [tool, getCanvasPosition, onElementSelect, onElementAdd, spread.id, spread.elements.length]\n  );\n\n  // Handle mouse move on canvas\n  const handleCanvasMouseMove = useCallback(\n    (e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n      const pos = getCanvasPosition(e);\n\n      // Update selection box\n      if (selectionBox.isSelecting) {\n        setSelectionBox((prev) => ({\n          ...prev,\n          currentX: pos.x,\n          currentY: pos.y,\n        }));\n      }\n\n      // Handle element drag\n      if (dragState.isDragging && dragState.elementId && dragState.originalPosition) {\n        const dx = pos.x - dragState.startX;\n        const dy = pos.y - dragState.startY;\n\n        onElementUpdate(dragState.elementId, {\n          x: dragState.originalPosition.x + dx,\n          y: dragState.originalPosition.y + dy,\n        });\n      }\n\n      // Handle element resize\n      if (resizeState.isResizing && resizeState.elementId && resizeState.originalBounds) {\n        const dx = pos.x - resizeState.startX;\n        const dy = pos.y - resizeState.startY;\n        const { x, y, width, height } = resizeState.originalBounds;\n        let newX = x;\n        let newY = y;\n        let newWidth = width;\n        let newHeight = height;\n\n        switch (resizeState.handle) {\n          case 'nw':\n            newX = x + dx;\n            newY = y + dy;\n            newWidth = width - dx;\n            newHeight = height - dy;\n            break;\n          case 'n':\n            newY = y + dy;\n            newHeight = height - dy;\n            break;\n          case 'ne':\n            newY = y + dy;\n            newWidth = width + dx;\n            newHeight = height - dy;\n            break;\n          case 'e':\n            newWidth = width + dx;\n            break;\n          case 'se':\n            newWidth = width + dx;\n            newHeight = height + dy;\n            break;\n          case 's':\n            newHeight = height + dy;\n            break;\n          case 'sw':\n            newX = x + dx;\n            newWidth = width - dx;\n            newHeight = height + dy;\n            break;\n          case 'w':\n            newX = x + dx;\n            newWidth = width - dx;\n            break;\n        }\n\n        // Enforce minimum size\n        const minSize = 20;\n        if (newWidth < minSize) {\n          newWidth = minSize;\n          if (resizeState.handle?.includes('w')) {\n            newX = x + width - minSize;\n          }\n        }\n        if (newHeight < minSize) {\n          newHeight = minSize;\n          if (resizeState.handle?.includes('n')) {\n            newY = y + height - minSize;\n          }\n        }\n\n        onElementUpdate(resizeState.elementId, {\n          x: newX,\n          y: newY,\n          width: newWidth,\n          height: newHeight,\n        });\n      }\n    },\n    [\n      getCanvasPosition,\n      selectionBox.isSelecting,\n      dragState,\n      resizeState,\n      onElementUpdate,\n    ]\n  );\n\n  // Handle mouse up on canvas\n  const handleCanvasMouseUp = useCallback(() => {\n    // Finalize selection box\n    if (selectionBox.isSelecting) {\n      const minX = Math.min(selectionBox.startX, selectionBox.currentX);\n      const maxX = Math.max(selectionBox.startX, selectionBox.currentX);\n      const minY = Math.min(selectionBox.startY, selectionBox.currentY);\n      const maxY = Math.max(selectionBox.startY, selectionBox.currentY);\n\n      // Find elements within selection box\n      const selectedIds = spread.elements\n        .filter((el) => {\n          const elRight = el.x + el.width;\n          const elBottom = el.y + el.height;\n          return el.x < maxX && elRight > minX && el.y < maxY && elBottom > minY;\n        })\n        .map((el) => el.id);\n\n      if (selectedIds.length > 0) {\n        onElementSelect(selectedIds);\n      }\n\n      setSelectionBox({\n        isSelecting: false,\n        startX: 0,\n        startY: 0,\n        currentX: 0,\n        currentY: 0,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // End drag\n    if (dragState.isDragging) {\n      setDragState({\n        isDragging: false,\n        startX: 0,\n        startY: 0,\n        elementId: null,\n        originalPosition: null,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // End resize\n    if (resizeState.isResizing) {\n      setResizeState({\n        isResizing: false,\n        handle: null,\n        elementId: null,\n        startX: 0,\n        startY: 0,\n        originalBounds: null,\n      });\n    }\n  }, [selectionBox, dragState, resizeState, spread.elements, onElementSelect]);\n\n  // Handle element click\n  const handleElementClick = useCallback(\n    (elementId: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n      e.stopPropagation();\n      if (e.shiftKey) {\n        // Toggle selection\n        if (selectedElementIds.includes(elementId)) {\n          onElementSelect(selectedElementIds.filter((id) => id !== elementId));\n        } else {\n          onElementSelect([...selectedElementIds, elementId]);\n        }\n      } else {\n        onElementSelect([elementId]);\n      }\n    },\n    [selectedElementIds, onElementSelect]\n  );\n\n  // Handle element drag start\n  const handleElementDragStart = useCallback(\n    (elementId: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n      const element = spread.elements.find((el) => el.id === elementId);\n      if (!element || element.locked) return;\n\n      const pos = getCanvasPosition(e);\n      setDragState({\n        isDragging: true,\n        startX: pos.x,\n        startY: pos.y,\n        elementId,\n        originalPosition: { x: element.x, y: element.y },\n      });\n    },\n    [spread.elements, getCanvasPosition]\n  );\n\n  // Handle element resize start\n  const handleElementResizeStart = useCallback(\n    (elementId: string, handle: ResizeState['handle'], e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n      const element = spread.elements.find((el) => el.id === elementId);\n      if (!element || element.locked) return;\n\n      e.stopPropagation();\n      const pos = getCanvasPosition(e);\n      setResizeState({\n        isResizing: true,\n        handle,\n        elementId,\n        startX: pos.x,\n        startY: pos.y,\n        originalBounds: {\n          x: element.x,\n          y: element.y,\n          width: element.width,\n          height: element.height,\n        },\n      });\n    },\n    [spread.elements, getCanvasPosition]\n  );\n\n  // Sort elements by z-index\n  const sortedElements = [...spread.elements].sort((a, b) => a.z_index - b.z_index);\n\n  return (\n    <div className={`relative flex items-center justify-center p-8 ${className}`}>\n      {/* Canvas */}\n      <div\n        ref={canvasRef}\n        className=\"relative bg-white shadow-2xl\"\n        style={{\n          width: spreadWidth * zoom,\n          height: spreadHeight * zoom,\n          backgroundColor: spread.background_color || '#ffffff',\n          backgroundImage: spread.background_image_url\n            ? `url(${spread.background_image_url})`\n            : undefined,\n          backgroundSize: 'cover',\n          backgroundPosition: 'center',\n        }}\n        onMouseDown={handleCanvasMouseDown}\n        onMouseMove={handleCanvasMouseMove}\n        onMouseUp={handleCanvasMouseUp}\n        onMouseLeave={handleCanvasMouseUp}\n      >\n        {/* Page Divider (for full spreads) */}\n        {spread.spread_type === 'full_spread' && (\n          <div\n            className=\"absolute top-0 bottom-0 border-l border-dashed border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 pointer-events-none\"\n            style={{ left: (spreadWidth * zoom) / 2 }}\n          />\n        )}\n\n        {/* Elements */}\n        {sortedElements.map((element) => (\n          <SpreadElement\n            key={element.id}\n            element={element}\n            zoom={zoom}\n            isSelected={selectedElementIds.includes(element.id)}\n            onClick={(e) => handleElementClick(element.id, e)}\n            onDragStart={(e) => handleElementDragStart(element.id, e)}\n            onResizeStart={(handle, e) => handleElementResizeStart(element.id, handle, e)}\n            collaboratorColor={\n              collaborators.find((c) => c.selected_elements.includes(element.id))?.color\n            }\n          />\n        ))}\n\n        {/* Selection Box */}\n        {selectionBox.isSelecting && (\n          <SelectionBox\n            startX={selectionBox.startX * zoom}\n            startY={selectionBox.startY * zoom}\n            endX={selectionBox.currentX * zoom}\n            endY={selectionBox.currentY * zoom}\n            color={myColor}\n          />\n        )}\n\n        {/* Collaborator Cursors */}\n        {collaborators.map(\n          (collaborator) =>\n            collaborator.cursor && (\n              <CollaboratorCursor\n                key={collaborator.user_id}\n                x={collaborator.cursor.x * zoom}\n                y={collaborator.cursor.y * zoom}\n                color={collaborator.color}\n                name={collaborator.display_name}\n              />\n            )\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default SpreadCanvas;\n"
}

  "content": "/**\n * SpreadElement Component\n *\n * Renders a single element on an album spread.\n * Handles different element types (photo, text, shape) and selection/resize handles.\n */\n\nimport React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport type { AlbumSpreadElement } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\nimport { ElementType } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface SpreadElementProps {\n  /** Element data */\n  element: AlbumSpreadElement;\n  /** Current zoom level */\n  zoom: number;\n  /** Whether the element is selected */\n  isSelected: boolean;\n  /** Click handler */\n  onClick: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void;\n  /** Drag start handler */\n  onDragStart: (e: React.MouseEvent) => void;\n  /** Resize start handler */\n  onResizeStart: (handle: 'nw' | 'n' | 'ne' | 'e' | 'se' | 's' | 'sw' | 'w', e: React.MouseEvent) => void;\n  /** Collaborator color if someone else is editing */\n  collaboratorColor?: string;\n}\n\ntype ResizeHandle = 'nw' | 'n' | 'ne' | 'e' | 'se' | 's' | 'sw' | 'w';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Resize Handles Component\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface ResizeHandlesProps {\n  onResizeStart: (handle: ResizeHandle, e: React.MouseEvent) => void;\n  color: string;\n}\n\nconst ResizeHandles: React.FC<ResizeHandlesProps> = ({ onResizeStart, color }) => {\n  const handles: { position: ResizeHandle; cursor: string; style: React.CSSProperties }[] = [\n    { position: 'nw', cursor: 'nw-resize', style: { top: -4, left: -4 } },\n    { position: 'n', cursor: 'n-resize', style: { top: -4, left: '50%', transform: 'translateX(-50%)' } },\n    { position: 'ne', cursor: 'ne-resize', style: { top: -4, right: -4 } },\n    { position: 'e', cursor: 'e-resize', style: { top: '50%', right: -4, transform: 'translateY(-50%)' } },\n    { position: 'se', cursor: 'se-resize', style: { bottom: -4, right: -4 } },\n    { position: 's', cursor: 's-resize', style: { bottom: -4, left: '50%', transform: 'translateX(-50%)' } },\n    { position: 'sw', cursor: 'sw-resize', style: { bottom: -4, left: -4 } },\n    { position: 'w', cursor: 'w-resize', style: { top: '50%', left: -4, transform: 'translateY(-50%)' } },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      {handles.map(({ position, cursor, style }) => (\n        <div\n          key={position}\n          className=\"absolute w-2 h-2 rounded-full border-2 bg-white\"\n          style={{\n            ...style,\n            borderColor: color,\n            cursor,\n          }}\n          onMouseDown={(e) => {\n            e.stopPropagation();\n            onResizeStart(position, e);\n          }}\n        />\n      ))}\n    </>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Element Content Components\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface PhotoElementContentProps {\n  element: AlbumSpreadElement;\n}\n\nconst PhotoElementContent: React.FC<PhotoElementContentProps> = ({ element }) => {\n  // In a real implementation, this would load the asset image\n  const imageUrl = element.asset_id ? `/api/v1/assets/${element.asset_id}/thumbnail` : undefined;\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"w-full h-full bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 overflow-hidden\"\n      style={{\n        borderRadius: element.style?.border_radius || 0,\n      }}\n    >\n      {imageUrl ? (\n        <img\n          src={imageUrl}\n          alt=\"\"\n          className=\"w-full h-full object-cover\"\n          style={{\n            filter: element.content?.filters\n              ? `\n                brightness(${element.content.filters.brightness || 1})\n                contrast(${element.content.filters.contrast || 1})\n                saturate(${element.content.filters.saturation || 1})\n                ${element.content.filters.grayscale ? 'grayscale(1)' : ''}\n                sepia(${element.content.filters.sepia || 0})\n                blur(${element.content.filters.blur || 0}px)\n              `\n              : undefined,\n          }}\n          draggable={false}\n        />\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400\">\n          <svg className=\"w-8 h-8\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path\n              strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n              strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n              strokeWidth={1.5}\n              d=\"M4 16l4.586-4.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L16 16m-2-2l1.586-1.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L20 14m-6-6h.01M6 20h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z\"\n            />\n          </svg>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\ninterface TextElementContentProps {\n  element: AlbumSpreadElement;\n}\n\nconst TextElementContent: React.FC<TextElementContentProps> = ({ element }) => {\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"w-full h-full p-2 overflow-hidden\"\n      style={{\n        fontFamily: element.style?.font_family || 'Inter',\n        fontSize: element.style?.font_size || 16,\n        fontWeight: element.style?.font_weight || 'normal',\n        fontStyle: element.style?.font_style || 'normal',\n        textAlign: element.style?.text_align || 'left',\n        lineHeight: element.style?.line_height || 1.5,\n        letterSpacing: element.style?.letter_spacing || 0,\n        color: element.style?.text_color || '#000000',\n      }}\n    >\n      {element.content?.html ? (\n        <div dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: element.content.html }} />\n      ) : (\n        element.content?.text || ''\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\ninterface ShapeElementContentProps {\n  element: AlbumSpreadElement;\n}\n\nconst ShapeElementContent: React.FC<ShapeElementContentProps> = ({ element }) => {\n  const shapeType = element.content?.shape_type || 'rectangle';\n  const fillColor = element.style?.fill_color || '#e5e7eb';\n  const strokeColor = element.style?.stroke_color;\n  const strokeWidth = element.style?.stroke_width || 0;\n\n  if (shapeType === 'rectangle') {\n    return (\n      <div\n        className=\"w-full h-full\"\n        style={{\n          backgroundColor: fillColor,\n          border: strokeWidth ? `${strokeWidth}px solid ${strokeColor}` : undefined,\n          borderRadius: element.style?.border_radius || 0,\n        }}\n      />\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (shapeType === 'ellipse') {\n    return (\n      <div\n        className=\"w-full h-full rounded-full\"\n        style={{\n          backgroundColor: fillColor,\n          border: strokeWidth ? `${strokeWidth}px solid ${strokeColor}` : undefined,\n        }}\n      />\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Custom SVG path\n  if (element.content?.path_data) {\n    return (\n      <svg className=\"w-full h-full\" viewBox=\"0 0 100 100\" preserveAspectRatio=\"none\">\n        <path\n          d={element.content.path_data}\n          fill={fillColor}\n          stroke={strokeColor}\n          strokeWidth={strokeWidth}\n        />\n      </svg>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return null;\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Main Component\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport const SpreadElement: React.FC<SpreadElementProps> = ({\n  element,\n  zoom,\n  isSelected,\n  onClick,\n  onDragStart,\n  onResizeStart,\n  collaboratorColor,\n}) => {\n  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false);\n\n  // Determine border color\n  const borderColor = collaboratorColor || (isSelected ? '#3b82f6' : 'transparent');\n  const showHandles = isSelected && !element.locked;\n\n  // Apply shadow if defined\n  const shadowStyle = element.style?.shadow\n    ? `${element.style.shadow.x}px ${element.style.shadow.y}px ${element.style.shadow.blur}px ${element.style.shadow.spread}px ${element.style.shadow.color}`\n    : undefined;\n\n  // Render element content based on type\n  const renderContent = () => {\n    switch (element.element_type) {\n      case ElementType.PHOTO:\n        return <PhotoElementContent element={element} />;\n      case ElementType.TEXT:\n        return <TextElementContent element={element} />;\n      case ElementType.SHAPE:\n      case ElementType.BACKGROUND:\n        return <ShapeElementContent element={element} />;\n      case ElementType.FRAME:\n        return <ShapeElementContent element={element} />;\n      case ElementType.EMBELLISHMENT:\n        return <ShapeElementContent element={element} />;\n      default:\n        return null;\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (!element.visible) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"absolute\"\n      style={{\n        left: element.x * zoom,\n        top: element.y * zoom,\n        width: element.width * zoom,\n        height: element.height * zoom,\n        transform: `rotate(${element.rotation}deg)`,\n        opacity: element.opacity,\n        boxShadow: shadowStyle,\n        cursor: element.locked ? 'not-allowed' : 'move',\n        outline: `2px solid ${borderColor}`,\n        outlineOffset: -2,\n        zIndex: element.z_index,\n      }}\n      onClick={onClick}\n      onMouseDown={(e) => {\n        if (!element.locked && e.button === 0) {\n          onDragStart(e);\n        }\n      }}\n      onMouseEnter={() => setIsHovered(true)}\n      onMouseLeave={() => setIsHovered(false)}\n    >\n      {/* Element Content */}\n      {renderContent()}\n\n      {/* Lock indicator */}\n      {element.locked && isHovered && (\n        <div className=\"absolute top-1 right-1 bg-black/50 text-white p-1 rounded\">\n          <svg className=\"w-3 h-3\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path\n              strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n              strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n              strokeWidth={2}\n              d=\"M12 15v2m-6 4h12a2 2 0 002-2v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2zm10-10V7a4 4 0 00-8 0v4h8z\"\n            />\n          </svg>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Collaborator indicator */}\n      {collaboratorColor && !isSelected && (\n        <div\n          className=\"absolute -top-6 left-0 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-white rounded\"\n          style={{ backgroundColor: collaboratorColor }}\n        >\n          Editing\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Resize Handles */}\n      {showHandles && <ResizeHandles onResizeStart={onResizeStart} color=\"#3b82f6\" />}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default SpreadElement;\n"
}

  "content": "/**\n * SpreadThumbnailStrip Component\n *\n * A vertical strip of spread thumbnails for navigation within an album.\n * Supports drag-and-drop reordering and shows collaborator indicators.\n */\n\nimport React, { useCallback, useState } from 'react';\nimport type { AlbumSpread, AlbumCollaboratorPresence } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\nimport { SpreadType } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface SpreadThumbnailStripProps {\n  /** Spreads in the album */\n  spreads: AlbumSpread[];\n  /** Currently selected spread index */\n  selectedIndex: number;\n  /** Callback when spread is selected */\n  onSelect: (index: number) => void;\n  /** Callback when spreads are reordered */\n  onReorder: (fromIndex: number, toIndex: number) => void;\n  /** Callback to add a spread */\n  onAdd: (position?: number) => void;\n  /** Callback to delete a spread */\n  onDelete: (spreadId: string) => void;\n  /** Active collaborators */\n  collaborators: AlbumCollaboratorPresence[];\n  /** Current user ID */\n  currentUserId: string;\n  /** Class name for container */\n  className?: string;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Spread Thumbnail Component\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface SpreadThumbnailProps {\n  spread: AlbumSpread;\n  index: number;\n  isSelected: boolean;\n  collaborators: AlbumCollaboratorPresence[];\n  onClick: () => void;\n  onDelete: () => void;\n  onDragStart: (e: React.DragEvent, index: number) => void;\n  onDragOver: (e: React.DragEvent) => void;\n  onDrop: (e: React.DragEvent, toIndex: number) => void;\n  isDragOver: boolean;\n}\n\nconst SpreadThumbnail: React.FC<SpreadThumbnailProps> = ({\n  spread,\n  index,\n  isSelected,\n  collaborators,\n  onClick,\n  onDelete,\n  onDragStart,\n  onDragOver,\n  onDrop,\n  isDragOver,\n}) => {\n  const [showMenu, setShowMenu] = useState(false);\n\n  // Get spread type label\n  const getSpreadLabel = () => {\n    switch (spread.spread_type) {\n      case SpreadType.COVER:\n        return 'Cover';\n      case SpreadType.BACK_COVER:\n        return 'Back';\n      case SpreadType.FULL_SPREAD:\n        return `${spread.page_numbers[0]}-${spread.page_numbers[1]}`;\n      case SpreadType.SINGLE_PAGE:\n        return `${spread.page_numbers[0]}`;\n      default:\n        return `${index + 1}`;\n    }\n  };\n\n  // Get collaborators on this spread\n  const spreadCollaborators = collaborators.filter(\n    (c) => c.current_spread_id === spread.id\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={`relative group cursor-pointer transition-all ${\n        isDragOver ? 'pt-12' : ''\n      }`}\n      draggable\n      onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, index)}\n      onDragOver={onDragOver}\n      onDrop={(e) => onDrop(e, index)}\n      onClick={onClick}\n      onContextMenu={(e) => {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        setShowMenu(true);\n      }}\n    >\n      {/* Drop indicator */}\n      {isDragOver && (\n        <div className=\"absolute top-0 left-2 right-2 h-1 bg-blue-500 rounded-full\" />\n      )}\n\n      {/* Thumbnail */}\n      <div\n        className={`relative mx-2 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border-2 transition-all ${\n          isSelected\n            ? 'border-blue-500 ring-2 ring-blue-500/30'\n            : 'border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 hover:border-gray-300 dark:hover:border-gray-600'\n        }`}\n      >\n        {/* Preview */}\n        <div\n          className=\"bg-white dark:bg-gray-800\"\n          style={{\n            aspectRatio: spread.spread_type === 'full_spread' ? '2/1' : '1/1',\n            backgroundColor: spread.background_color || '#ffffff',\n            backgroundImage: spread.background_image_url\n              ? `url(${spread.background_image_url})`\n              : undefined,\n            backgroundSize: 'cover',\n          }}\n        >\n          {/* Mini element previews */}\n          <div className=\"relative w-full h-full\">\n            {spread.elements.slice(0, 5).map((el) => (\n              <div\n                key={el.id}\n                className=\"absolute bg-gray-300 dark:bg-gray-600 rounded-sm\"\n                style={{\n                  left: `${(el.x / 1000) * 100}%`,\n                  top: `${(el.y / 1000) * 100}%`,\n                  width: `${(el.width / 1000) * 100}%`,\n                  height: `${(el.height / 1000) * 100}%`,\n                }}\n              />\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Collaborator indicators */}\n        {spreadCollaborators.length > 0 && (\n          <div className=\"absolute top-1 right-1 flex -space-x-1\">\n            {spreadCollaborators.slice(0, 3).map((collaborator) => (\n              <div\n                key={collaborator.user_id}\n                className=\"w-4 h-4 rounded-full border border-white dark:border-gray-900 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-[8px] font-bold\"\n                style={{ backgroundColor: collaborator.color }}\n                title={collaborator.display_name}\n              >\n                {collaborator.display_name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}\n              </div>\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Delete button (on hover) */}\n        <button\n          className=\"absolute top-1 left-1 p-0.5 bg-red-500 text-white rounded opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity\"\n          onClick={(e) => {\n            e.stopPropagation();\n            onDelete();\n          }}\n          title=\"Delete spread\"\n        >\n          <svg className=\"w-3 h-3\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12\" />\n          </svg>\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Label */}\n      <div className=\"text-center mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n        {getSpreadLabel()}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Context menu */}\n      {showMenu && (\n        <>\n          <div\n            className=\"fixed inset-0 z-40\"\n            onClick={() => setShowMenu(false)}\n          />\n          <div className=\"absolute left-full top-0 ml-1 z-50 bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg shadow-lg border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 py-1 w-36\">\n            <button\n              className=\"w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-left text-sm hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700\"\n              onClick={() => {\n                // Duplicate spread\n                setShowMenu(false);\n              }}\n            >\n              Duplicate\n            </button>\n            <button\n              className=\"w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-left text-sm text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-900/20\"\n              onClick={() => {\n                onDelete();\n                setShowMenu(false);\n              }}\n            >\n              Delete\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Main Component\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport const SpreadThumbnailStrip: React.FC<SpreadThumbnailStripProps> = ({\n  spreads,\n  selectedIndex,\n  onSelect,\n  onReorder,\n  onAdd,\n  onDelete,\n  collaborators,\n  currentUserId,\n  className = '',\n}) => {\n  const [dragIndex, setDragIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);\n  const [dropIndex, setDropIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);\n\n  const handleDragStart = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent, index: number) => {\n    setDragIndex(index);\n    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';\n    e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', String(index));\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'move';\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleDrop = useCallback(\n    (e: React.DragEvent, toIndex: number) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      if (dragIndex !== null && dragIndex !== toIndex) {\n        onReorder(dragIndex, toIndex);\n      }\n      setDragIndex(null);\n      setDropIndex(null);\n    },\n    [dragIndex, onReorder]\n  );\n\n  const handleDragEnter = useCallback((index: number) => {\n    setDropIndex(index);\n  }, []);\n\n  const handleDragEnd = useCallback(() => {\n    setDragIndex(null);\n    setDropIndex(null);\n  }, []);\n\n  // Filter out current user from collaborators\n  const otherCollaborators = collaborators.filter((c) => c.user_id !== currentUserId);\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={`flex flex-col bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900 overflow-hidden ${className}`}\n    >\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800\">\n        <span className=\"text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300\">\n          Spreads\n        </span>\n        <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n          {spreads.length}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Thumbnail List */}\n      <div\n        className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto py-2 space-y-2\"\n        onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}\n      >\n        {spreads.map((spread, index) => (\n          <SpreadThumbnail\n            key={spread.id}\n            spread={spread}\n            index={index}\n            isSelected={index === selectedIndex}\n            collaborators={otherCollaborators}\n            onClick={() => onSelect(index)}\n            onDelete={() => onDelete(spread.id)}\n            onDragStart={handleDragStart}\n            onDragOver={(e) => {\n              handleDragOver(e);\n              handleDragEnter(index);\n            }}\n            onDrop={handleDrop}\n            isDragOver={dropIndex === index && dragIndex !== index}\n          />\n        ))}\n\n        {/* Add Spread Button */}\n        <button\n          className=\"mx-2 p-3 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-700 rounded-lg text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:hover:text-gray-300 hover:border-gray-400 dark:hover:border-gray-600 transition-colors\"\n          onClick={() => onAdd()}\n        >\n          <svg className=\"w-5 h-5 mx-auto\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M12 4v16m8-8H4\" />\n          </svg>\n          <span className=\"sr-only\">Add spread</span>\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default SpreadThumbnailStrip;\n"
}

  "content": "/**\n * AlbumToolbar Component\n *\n * Top toolbar for album editor with tools, zoom controls, and actions.\n */\n\nimport React from 'react';\nimport type { Album } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface AlbumToolbarProps {\n  /** Album data */\n  album: Album;\n  /** Currently active tool */\n  tool: 'select' | 'pan' | 'text' | 'shape';\n  /** Callback when tool changes */\n  onToolChange: (tool: 'select' | 'pan' | 'text' | 'shape') => void;\n  /** Current zoom level */\n  zoom: number;\n  /** Callback when zoom changes */\n  onZoomChange: (zoom: number) => void;\n  /** Callback to undo */\n  onUndo: () => void;\n  /** Callback to redo */\n  onRedo: () => void;\n  /** Whether undo is available */\n  canUndo: boolean;\n  /** Whether redo is available */\n  canRedo: boolean;\n  /** Callback to add a spread */\n  onAddSpread: () => void;\n  /** Whether properties panel is visible */\n  showProperties: boolean;\n  /** Callback to toggle properties panel */\n  onToggleProperties: () => void;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Tool Button Component\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface ToolButtonProps {\n  icon: React.ReactNode;\n  label: string;\n  isActive: boolean;\n  onClick: () => void;\n  shortcut?: string;\n}\n\nconst ToolButton: React.FC<ToolButtonProps> = ({\n  icon,\n  label,\n  isActive,\n  onClick,\n  shortcut,\n}) => (\n  <button\n    className={`p-2 rounded-lg transition-colors ${\n      isActive\n        ? 'bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400'\n        : 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800'\n    }`}\n    onClick={onClick}\n    title={shortcut ? `${label} (${shortcut})` : label}\n  >\n    {icon}\n  </button>\n);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Main Component\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport const AlbumToolbar: React.FC<AlbumToolbarProps> = ({\n  album,\n  tool,\n  onToolChange,\n  zoom,\n  onZoomChange,\n  onUndo,\n  onRedo,\n  canUndo,\n  canRedo,\n  onAddSpread,\n  showProperties,\n  onToggleProperties,\n}) => {\n  const zoomPresets = [0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1, 1.25, 1.5, 2];\n\n  const handleZoomIn = () => {\n    const nextZoom = zoomPresets.find((z) => z > zoom) || zoom;\n    onZoomChange(nextZoom);\n  };\n\n  const handleZoomOut = () => {\n    const nextZoom = [...zoomPresets].reverse().find((z) => z < zoom) || zoom;\n    onZoomChange(nextZoom);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-2 bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800\">\n      {/* Left: Album Title & Tools */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-4\">\n        {/* Album Title */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <h1 className=\"text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100\">\n            {album.name}\n          </h1>\n          <span className=\"px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 rounded\">\n            {album.status}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Divider */}\n        <div className=\"h-6 w-px bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700\" />\n\n        {/* Tool Buttons */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1\">\n          <ToolButton\n            icon={\n              <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n                <path\n                  strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                  strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                  strokeWidth={2}\n                  d=\"M15 15l-2 5L9 9l11 4-5 2zm0 0l5 5M7.188 2.239l.777 2.897M5.136 7.965l-2.898-.777M13.95 4.05l-2.122 2.122m-5.657 5.656l-2.12 2.122\"\n                />\n              </svg>\n            }\n            label=\"Select\"\n            isActive={tool === 'select'}\n            onClick={() => onToolChange('select')}\n            shortcut=\"V\"\n          />\n          <ToolButton\n            icon={\n              <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n                <path\n                  strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                  strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                  strokeWidth={2}\n                  d=\"M7 11.5V14m0-2.5v-6a1.5 1.5 0 113 0m-3 6a1.5 1.5 0 00-3 0v2a7.5 7.5 0 0015 0v-5a1.5 1.5 0 00-3 0m-6-3V11m0-5.5v-1a1.5 1.5 0 013 0v1m0 0V11m0-5.5a1.5 1.5 0 013 0v3m0 0V11\"\n                />\n              </svg>\n            }\n            label=\"Pan\"\n            isActive={tool === 'pan'}\n            onClick={() => onToolChange('pan')}\n            shortcut=\"H\"\n          />\n          <ToolButton\n            icon={\n              <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n                <path\n                  strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                  strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                  strokeWidth={2}\n                  d=\"M11 5H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v11a2 2 0 002 2h11a2 2 0 002-2v-5m-1.414-9.414a2 2 0 112.828 2.828L11.828 15H9v-2.828l8.586-8.586z\"\n                />\n              </svg>\n            }\n            label=\"Text\"\n            isActive={tool === 'text'}\n            onClick={() => onToolChange('text')}\n            shortcut=\"T\"\n          />\n          <ToolButton\n            icon={\n              <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n                <path\n                  strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                  strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                  strokeWidth={2}\n                  d=\"M4 5a1 1 0 011-1h14a1 1 0 011 1v2a1 1 0 01-1 1H5a1 1 0 01-1-1V5zM4 13a1 1 0 011-1h6a1 1 0 011 1v6a1 1 0 01-1 1H5a1 1 0 01-1-1v-6z\"\n                />\n              </svg>\n            }\n            label=\"Shape\"\n            isActive={tool === 'shape'}\n            onClick={() => onToolChange('shape')}\n            shortcut=\"R\"\n          />\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Divider */}\n        <div className=\"h-6 w-px bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700\" />\n\n        {/* Undo/Redo */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1\">\n          <button\n            className={`p-2 rounded-lg transition-colors ${\n              canUndo\n                ? 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800'\n                : 'text-gray-300 dark:text-gray-600 cursor-not-allowed'\n            }`}\n            onClick={onUndo}\n            disabled={!canUndo}\n            title=\"Undo (Ctrl+Z)\"\n          >\n            <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n              <path\n                strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                strokeWidth={2}\n                d=\"M3 10h10a8 8 0 018 8v2M3 10l6 6m-6-6l6-6\"\n              />\n            </svg>\n          </button>\n          <button\n            className={`p-2 rounded-lg transition-colors ${\n              canRedo\n                ? 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800'\n                : 'text-gray-300 dark:text-gray-600 cursor-not-allowed'\n            }`}\n            onClick={onRedo}\n            disabled={!canRedo}\n            title=\"Redo (Ctrl+Shift+Z)\"\n          >\n            <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n              <path\n                strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                strokeWidth={2}\n                d=\"M21 10h-10a8 8 0 00-8 8v2M21 10l-6 6m6-6l-6-6\"\n              />\n            </svg>\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Right: Actions & Zoom */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center gap-4\">\n        {/* Add Spread Button */}\n        <button\n          className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg transition-colors\"\n          onClick={onAddSpread}\n        >\n          <svg className=\"w-4 h-4\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M12 4v16m8-8H4\" />\n          </svg>\n          Add Spread\n        </button>\n\n        {/* Divider */}\n        <div className=\"h-6 w-px bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700\" />\n\n        {/* Zoom Controls */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          <button\n            className=\"p-1.5 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 rounded transition-colors\"\n            onClick={handleZoomOut}\n            title=\"Zoom out\"\n          >\n            <svg className=\"w-4 h-4\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n              <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M20 12H4\" />\n            </svg>\n          </button>\n\n          <select\n            value={zoom}\n            onChange={(e) => onZoomChange(Number(e.target.value))}\n            className=\"px-2 py-1 text-sm bg-transparent text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded\"\n          >\n            {zoomPresets.map((z) => (\n              <option key={z} value={z}>\n                {Math.round(z * 100)}%\n              </option>\n            ))}\n          </select>\n\n          <button\n            className=\"p-1.5 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 rounded transition-colors\"\n            onClick={handleZoomIn}\n            title=\"Zoom in\"\n          >\n            <svg className=\"w-4 h-4\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n              <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M12 4v16m8-8H4\" />\n            </svg>\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Divider */}\n        <div className=\"h-6 w-px bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700\" />\n\n        {/* Toggle Properties Panel */}\n        <button\n          className={`p-2 rounded-lg transition-colors ${\n            showProperties\n              ? 'bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300'\n              : 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800'\n          }`}\n          onClick={onToggleProperties}\n          title=\"Toggle properties panel\"\n        >\n          <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path\n              strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n              strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n              strokeWidth={2}\n              d=\"M12 6V4m0 2a2 2 0 100 4m0-4a2 2 0 110 4m-6 8a2 2 0 100-4m0 4a2 2 0 110-4m0 4v2m0-6V4m6 6v10m6-2a2 2 0 100-4m0 4a2 2 0 110-4m0 4v2m0-6V4\"\n            />\n          </svg>\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default AlbumToolbar;\n"
}

  "content": "/**\n * ElementPropertiesPanel Component\n *\n * Right panel for editing properties of selected elements or the spread.\n */\n\nimport React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport type { AlbumSpread, AlbumSpreadElement } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\nimport { ElementType } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface ElementPropertiesPanelProps {\n  /** Selected elements */\n  elements: AlbumSpreadElement[];\n  /** Current spread */\n  spread?: AlbumSpread;\n  /** Callback when element is updated */\n  onElementUpdate: (elementId: string, updates: Partial<AlbumSpreadElement>) => void;\n  /** Callback when spread is updated */\n  onSpreadUpdate: (spreadId: string, updates: Partial<AlbumSpread>) => void;\n  /** Class name for container */\n  className?: string;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Property Input Components\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface NumberInputProps {\n  label: string;\n  value: number;\n  onChange: (value: number) => void;\n  min?: number;\n  max?: number;\n  step?: number;\n  unit?: string;\n}\n\nconst NumberInput: React.FC<NumberInputProps> = ({\n  label,\n  value,\n  onChange,\n  min,\n  max,\n  step = 1,\n  unit,\n}) => (\n  <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    <label className=\"text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400\">{label}</label>\n    <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1\">\n      <input\n        type=\"number\"\n        value={value}\n        onChange={(e) => onChange(Number(e.target.value))}\n        min={min}\n        max={max}\n        step={step}\n        className=\"w-20 px-2 py-1 text-sm text-right bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-transparent\"\n      />\n      {unit && <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">{unit}</span>}\n    </div>\n  </div>\n);\n\ninterface ColorInputProps {\n  label: string;\n  value: string;\n  onChange: (value: string) => void;\n}\n\nconst ColorInput: React.FC<ColorInputProps> = ({ label, value, onChange }) => (\n  <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    <label className=\"text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400\">{label}</label>\n    <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n      <input\n        type=\"color\"\n        value={value}\n        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n        className=\"w-8 h-8 p-0 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded cursor-pointer\"\n      />\n      <input\n        type=\"text\"\n        value={value}\n        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n        className=\"w-20 px-2 py-1 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded\"\n      />\n    </div>\n  </div>\n);\n\ninterface SelectInputProps {\n  label: string;\n  value: string;\n  options: { value: string; label: string }[];\n  onChange: (value: string) => void;\n}\n\nconst SelectInput: React.FC<SelectInputProps> = ({ label, value, options, onChange }) => (\n  <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between\">\n    <label className=\"text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400\">{label}</label>\n    <select\n      value={value}\n      onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}\n      className=\"w-32 px-2 py-1 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded\"\n    >\n      {options.map((opt) => (\n        <option key={opt.value} value={opt.value}>\n          {opt.label}\n        </option>\n      ))}\n    </select>\n  </div>\n);\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Property Sections\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface TransformSectionProps {\n  element: AlbumSpreadElement;\n  onUpdate: (updates: Partial<AlbumSpreadElement>) => void;\n}\n\nconst TransformSection: React.FC<TransformSectionProps> = ({ element, onUpdate }) => (\n  <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n    <h3 className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider\">\n      Transform\n    </h3>\n    <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n      <NumberInput label=\"X\" value={Math.round(element.x)} onChange={(x) => onUpdate({ x })} unit=\"px\" />\n      <NumberInput label=\"Y\" value={Math.round(element.y)} onChange={(y) => onUpdate({ y })} unit=\"px\" />\n      <NumberInput\n        label=\"W\"\n        value={Math.round(element.width)}\n        onChange={(width) => onUpdate({ width })}\n        min={10}\n        unit=\"px\"\n      />\n      <NumberInput\n        label=\"H\"\n        value={Math.round(element.height)}\n        onChange={(height) => onUpdate({ height })}\n        min={10}\n        unit=\"px\"\n      />\n    </div>\n    <NumberInput\n      label=\"Rotation\"\n      value={element.rotation}\n      onChange={(rotation) => onUpdate({ rotation })}\n      min={-360}\n      max={360}\n      unit=\"deg\"\n    />\n    <NumberInput\n      label=\"Opacity\"\n      value={element.opacity * 100}\n      onChange={(opacity) => onUpdate({ opacity: opacity / 100 })}\n      min={0}\n      max={100}\n      unit=\"%\"\n    />\n  </div>\n);\n\ninterface TextStyleSectionProps {\n  element: AlbumSpreadElement;\n  onUpdate: (updates: Partial<AlbumSpreadElement>) => void;\n}\n\nconst TextStyleSection: React.FC<TextStyleSectionProps> = ({ element, onUpdate }) => {\n  const style = element.style || {};\n\n  const fontFamilies = [\n    { value: 'Inter', label: 'Inter' },\n    { value: 'Georgia', label: 'Georgia' },\n    { value: 'Times New Roman', label: 'Times New Roman' },\n    { value: 'Arial', label: 'Arial' },\n    { value: 'Helvetica', label: 'Helvetica' },\n    { value: 'Playfair Display', label: 'Playfair Display' },\n    { value: 'Lora', label: 'Lora' },\n    { value: 'Montserrat', label: 'Montserrat' },\n  ];\n\n  const textAlignOptions = [\n    { value: 'left', label: 'Left' },\n    { value: 'center', label: 'Center' },\n    { value: 'right', label: 'Right' },\n    { value: 'justify', label: 'Justify' },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n      <h3 className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider\">\n        Typography\n      </h3>\n      <SelectInput\n        label=\"Font\"\n        value={style.font_family || 'Inter'}\n        options={fontFamilies}\n        onChange={(font_family) => onUpdate({ style: { ...style, font_family } })}\n      />\n      <NumberInput\n        label=\"Size\"\n        value={style.font_size || 16}\n        onChange={(font_size) => onUpdate({ style: { ...style, font_size } })}\n        min={8}\n        max={200}\n        unit=\"px\"\n      />\n      <SelectInput\n        label=\"Align\"\n        value={style.text_align || 'left'}\n        options={textAlignOptions}\n        onChange={(text_align) =>\n          onUpdate({ style: { ...style, text_align: text_align as 'left' | 'center' | 'right' | 'justify' } })\n        }\n      />\n      <ColorInput\n        label=\"Color\"\n        value={style.text_color || '#000000'}\n        onChange={(text_color) => onUpdate({ style: { ...style, text_color } })}\n      />\n      <NumberInput\n        label=\"Line Height\"\n        value={style.line_height || 1.5}\n        onChange={(line_height) => onUpdate({ style: { ...style, line_height } })}\n        min={0.5}\n        max={3}\n        step={0.1}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\ninterface FillStrokeSectionProps {\n  element: AlbumSpreadElement;\n  onUpdate: (updates: Partial<AlbumSpreadElement>) => void;\n}\n\nconst FillStrokeSection: React.FC<FillStrokeSectionProps> = ({ element, onUpdate }) => {\n  const style = element.style || {};\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n      <h3 className=\"text-xs font-semibold },\n  };\n\n  const { color, text, pulse } = config[status];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n      <span className=\"relative flex h-2 w-2\">\n        {pulse && (\n collaborator.display_name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Main Component\n// 'rotate-180' : ''\n                }`}\n                fill=\"none\"\n                viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\"\n                stroke=\"currentColor\"\n              >\n                <path\n                  strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                  strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                  strokeWidth={2}\n                  d=\"M19 9l-7 7-7-7\"\n                />\n              </svg>\n            </button>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Right: Leave button */}\n        <button\n          onClick={onStopCollaboration}\n          className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 {otherCollaborators.map((collaborator) => (\n                <div\n                  key={collaborator.user_id}\n                  className=\"flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2 hover:bg-gray-50 <p className=\"text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 truncate\">\n                      {collaborator.display_name}\n                    </p>\n                    <p className=\"text-xs   "content": "/**\n * SelectionBox Component\n *\n * Displays a selection rectangle when dragging to select multiple elements.\n */\n\nimport React from 'react';\n\ninterface SelectionBoxProps {\n  /** Start X position */\n  startX: number;\n  /** Start Y position */\n  startY: number;\n  /** End X position */\n  endX: number;\n  /** End Y position */\n  endY: number;\n  /** Selection color */\n  color: string;\n}\n\nexport const SelectionBox: React.FC<SelectionBoxProps> = ({\n  startX,\n  startY,\n  endX,\n  endY,\n  color,\n}) => {\n  const left = Math.min(startX, endX);\n  const top = Math.min(startY, endY);\n  const width = Math.abs(endX - startX);\n  const height = Math.abs(endY - startY);\n\n  if (width < 2 && height < 2) {\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"absolute pointer-events-none\"\n      style={{\n        left,\n        top,\n        width,\n        height,\n        backgroundColor: `${color}20`,\n        border: `1px dashed ${color}`,\n      }}\n    />\n  );\n};\n\nexport default SelectionBox;\n"
}

*/\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';\nimport type {\n  Album,\n  AlbumSpread,\n  AlbumSpreadElement,\n  AlbumCollaborationSession,\n  AlbumCollaboratorPresence,\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface status */\n  connectionStatus: 'disconnected' | 'connecting' | 'connected';\n  /** Current session */\n  session: AlbumCollaborationSession | null;\n  /** Active collaborators */\n  collaborators: collaborator.user_id));\n        } else if (event === 'updated') {\n          setCollaborators((prev) =>\n            prev.map((c) => (c.user_id === collaborator.user_id ? collaborator : c))\n [mergeVectorClock]);\n\n  // Start collaboration\n  const startCollaboration = useCallback(async () => {\n    setIsCollaborative(true);\n    await connectWebSocket();\n  }, [connectWebSocket]);\n\n          onClick={() => setPreviewVersion(null)}\n          />\n          <div className=\"fixed inset-8 bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 rounded-xl shadow-xl border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 z-50 flex flex-col overflow-hidden\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800\">\n              <h3 className=\"font-semibold <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                <button\n                  onClick={() => {\n                    onRollback(previewVersion.id);\n                    setPreviewVersion(null);\n                  }}\n                  className=\"px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 rounded-lg transition-colors\"\n                >\n                  Restore This Version\n                </button>\n                <button\n                  onClick={() => setPreviewVersion(null)}\n                  className=\"p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 dark:hover:text-gray-300 rounded\"\n                >\n                  <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n                    <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12\" />\n                  </svg>\n                </button>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"flex-1 overflow-auto p-8 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-950\">\n              {/* Preview content would render the snapshot here */}\n              <div className=\"text-center text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n                Preview rendering would go here\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default VersionHistoryPanel;\n"

 /** Comments list */\n  comments: AlbumComment[];\n  /** Currently selected spread ID */\n  selectedSpreadId?: string;\n  /** Currently selected element ID */\n  selectedElementId?: string;\n  /** Available users for @mentions */\n  availableUsers: MentionedUser[];\n  /** Whether comments are loading */\n  isLoading: boolean;\n  /** Callback to create a comment */\n  onCreateComment: (content: setMentionStartIndex] = useState(-1);\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);\n\n  const handleInput = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {\n    const newValue = e.target.value;\n    const cursorPos = e.target.selectionStart || 0;\n\n    onChange(newValue);\n\n    // Check for @ mention trigger\n    const textBeforeCursor = newValue.slice(0, cursorPos);\n    const lastAtIndex = textBeforeCursor.lastIndexOf('@');\n\n    if (lastAtIndex >= 0) {\n      const query = textBeforeCursor.slice(lastAtIndex + 1);\n      // Only show suggestions if there's no space after @\n      if (!query.includes(' ') && query.length < 20) {\n        setMentionQuery(query.toLowerCase());\n        setMentionStartIndex(lastAtIndex);\n        setShowSuggestions(true);\n        return;\n      }\n    }\n\n    setShowSuggestions(false);\n  };\n\n  const handleSelectUser = (user: MentionedUser) => {\n    const before = value.slice(0, mentionStartIndex);\n    const after = value.slice((inputRef.current?.selectionStart || 0));\n    const newValue = `${before}@${user.name} ${after}`;\n    onChange(newValue);\n    onMention(user);\n    setShowSuggestions(false);\n\n    // Focus back to input\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      if (inputRef.current) {\n        const newCursorPos = mentionStartIndex + user.name.length + 2;\n        inputRef.current.focus();\n        inputRef.current.setSelectionRange(newCursorPos, newCursorPos);\n      }\n    }, 0);\n  };\n\n  const filteredUsers = availableUsers.filter(\n    (user) =>\n      user.name.toLowerCase().includes(mentionQuery) ||\n      user.email?.toLowerCase().includes(mentionQuery)\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative\">\n      <textarea\n        ref={inputRef}\n        value={value}\n        onChange={handleInput}\n        onBlur={() => setTimeout(() => setShowSuggestions(false), 200)}\n        placeholder={placeholder}\n        className=\"w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-lg resize-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-transparent\"\n        rows={3}\n      />\n\n      {/* Mention Suggestions */}\n      {showSuggestions && filteredUsers.length > 0 && (\n        <div className=\"absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-1 w-full max-h-40 key={user.id}\n              onClick={() => handleSelectUser(user)}\n              className=\"w-full flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 text-left hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-700\"\n            >\n              <div className=\"w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-blue-500 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-medium\">\n                {user.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n                <p className=\"text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100 truncate\">\n                  {user.name}\n                </p>\n                {user.email && (\n                  <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 truncate\">\n                    {user.email}\n                  </p>\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </button>\n          ))}\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// useState(false);\n\n  const formatTime = (dateString: string) => {\n    const date = new Date(dateString);\n    const now = new Date();\n    const diff = now.getTime() - date.getTime();\n    const minutes = Math.floor(diff / (1000 * 60));\n    const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);\n    const days = Math.floor(hours / 24);\n\n    if (minutes < 1) return 'Just now';\n    if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;\n    if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;\n    if (days < 7) return `${days}d ago`;\n    return date.toLocaleDateString();\n  };\n\n  // Render content with highlighted const mentionRegex = /@(\\w+)/g;\n    let match;\n\n    while ((match = mentionRegex.exec(content)) !== null) {\n      // Add text before mention\n      if (match.index > lastIndex) {\n        parts.push(content.slice(lastIndex, match.index));\n      }\n\n      // Add highlighted mention\n      const mentionedUser = comment.mentioned_users.find(\n        (u) => u.name.toLowerCase().replace(/\\s+/g, '') === match[1].toLowerCase()\n      );\n\n      parts.push(\n        <span\n          key={match.index}\n          className={`font-medium ${\n            mentionedUser\n              ? 'text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400'\n              : 'text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100'\n          }`}\n        >\n          @{match[1]}\n        </span>\n      );\n\n      lastIndex = match.index + match[0].length;\n    }\n\n    // Add remaining text\n    if (lastIndex < content.length) {\n      parts.push(content.slice(lastIndex));\n    }\n\n    return parts;\n  };\n\n  const isResolved = comment.status === 'resolved' as CommentStatus;\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={`p-3 rounded-lg border transition-all cursor-pointer ${\n        isResolved\n          ? 'border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800 bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-900/50 opacity-60'\n          : 'border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 hover:border-gray-300 dark:hover:border-gray-600'\n      }`}\n      onClick={onClick}\n      onMouseEnter={() => setShowActions(true)}\n      onMouseLeave={() => setShowActions(false)}\n    >\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-2\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n          {comment.author_avatar ? (\n            <img\n              src={comment.author_avatar}\n              alt={comment.author_name}\n              className=\"w-6 h-6 rounded-full\"\n            />\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-blue-500 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-medium\">\n              {comment.author_name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}\n            </div>\n          )}\n          <span className=\"text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-gray-100\">\n            {comment.author_name}\n          </span>\n          <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n            {formatTime(comment.created_at)}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Status Badge */}\n        {isResolved && (\n          <span className=\"px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-green-600 dark:text-green-400 bg-green-100 dark:bg-green-900/30 rounded\">\n            Resolved\n          </span>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Content */}\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 whitespace-pre-wrap\">\n        {renderContent()}\n      </p>\n\n      {/* Location Badge */}\n      {(comment.spread_id || comment.element_id) && (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 mt-2 text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n          <svg className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path\n              strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n              strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n              strokeWidth={2}\n              d=\"M17.657 16.657L13.414 20.9a1.998 1.998 0 01-2.827 0l-4.244-4.243a8 8 0 1111.314 0z\"\n            />\n          </svg>\n          {comment.element_id ? 'On element' : 'On spread'}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Replies Count */}\n      {comment.replies_count > 0 && (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-1 mt-2 text-xs text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400\">\n          <svg className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path\n              strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n              strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n              strokeWidth={2}\n              d=\"M8 12h.01M12 12h.01M16 12h.01M21 12c0 4.418-4.03 8-9 8a9.863 9.863 0 01-4.255-.949L3 20l1.395-3.72C3.512 15.042 3 13.574 3 12c0-4.418 4.03-8 9-8s9 3.582 9 8z\"\n            />\n          </svg>\n          {comment.replies_count} {comment.replies_count === 1 ? 'reply' : 'replies'}\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Actions */}\n      {showActions && !isResolved && (\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n          <button\n            onClick={(e) => {\n              e.stopPropagation();\n              onResolve();\n            }}\n            className=\"flex-1 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-green-600 dark:text-green-400 hover:bg-green-50 dark:hover:bg-green-900/20 rounded transition-colors\"\n          >\n            Resolve\n          </button>\n          <button\n            onClick={(e) => {\n              e.stopPropagation();\n              onDelete();\n            }}\n            className=\"px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-900/20 rounded transition-colors\"\n          >\n            Delete\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Main Component\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport const CommentPanel: React.FC<CommentPanelProps> = ({\n  comments,\n  selectedSpreadId,\n  selectedElementId,\n  availableUsers,\n  isLoading,\n  onCreateComment,\n  onResolveComment,\n  onDeleteComment,\n  onCommentClick,\n  onClose,\n  className = '',\n}) => {\n  const [newComment, setNewComment] = useState('');\n  const [mentionedUsers, setMentionedUsers] = useState<MentionedUser[]>([]);\n  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<'all' | 'open' | 'resolved'>('all');\n\n  const handleSubmit = () => {\n    if (!newComment.trim()) return;\n\n    onCreateComment(\n      newComment.trim(),\n      mentionedUsers.map((u) => u.id)\n    );\n\n    setNewComment('');\n    setMentionedUsers([]);\n  };\n\n  const handleMention = useCallback((user: MentionedUser) => {\n comments.filter((comment) => {\n    if (filterStatus === 'open' && comment.status !== 'open') return false;\n    if (filterStatus === 'resolved' && comment.status !== 'resolved') return false;\n    return true;\n  });\n\n  // Separate thread comments from general comments\n  const threadComments = filteredComments.filter(\n    (c) =>\n      (selectedSpreadId && c.spread_id === selectedSpreadId)   <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800\">\n        <h2 className=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 />\n          </svg>\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Filter Tabs */}\n      <div className=\"flex px-4 py-2 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800 gap-2\">\n        {(['all', 'open', 'resolved'] as const).map((status) => (\n          <button\n            key={status}\n            onClick={() => setFilterStatus(status)}\n            className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium rounded-full transition-colors ${\n              filterStatus === status\n                ? 'bg-blue-100 dark:bg-blue-900/30 text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400'\n                : 'text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800'\n            }`}\n          >\n            {status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + status.slice(1)}\n            {status === 'all' && ` (${comments.length})`}\n            {status === 'open' && ` (${comments.filter((c) => c.status === 'open').length})`}\n            {status === 'resolved' && ` (${comments.filter((c) => c.status === 'resolved').length})`}\n          </button>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Comment Input */}\n      <div className=\"p-4 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-800\">\n        <MentionInput\n          value={newComment}\n          onChange={setNewComment}\n          availableUsers={availableUsers}\n          onMention={handleMention}\n          placeholder=\"Add a comment... Use @ to mention someone\"\n        />\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between mt-2\">\n          <div className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n            {selectedElementId\n              ? 'Commenting on selected element'\n              : selectedSpreadId\n              ? 'Commenting on current spread'\n              : 'General comment'}\n          </div>\n          <button\n            onClick={handleSubmit}\n            disabled={!newComment.trim()}\n            className=\"px-4 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-white bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:bg-gray-300 disabled:cursor-not-allowed rounded-lg transition-colors\"\n          >\n            Post\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Comment List */}\n      <div className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4\">\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center py-8\">\n            <div className=\"animate-spin rounded-full h-6 w-6 border-b-2 border-blue-500\" />\n          </div>\n        ) : filteredComments.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"text-center py-8\">\n            <div className=\"text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-600 mb-2\">\n              <svg\n                className=\"w-12 h-12 mx-auto\"\n                fill=\"none\"\n                viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\"\n                stroke=\"currentColor\"\n              >\n                <path\n                  strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                  strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                  strokeWidth={1.5}\n                  d=\"M8 12h.01M12 12h.01M16 12h.01M21 12c0 4.418-4.03 8-9 8a9.863 9.863 0 01-4.255-.949L3 20l1.395-3.72C3.512 15.042 3 13.574 3 12c0-4.418 4.03-8 9-8s9 3.582 9 8z\"\n                />\n              </svg>\n            </div>\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n              No comments yet\n            </p>\n            <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-gray-500 mt-1\">\n              Be the first to leave feedback\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            {/* Thread Comments (for selected element/spread) */}\n            {threadComments.length > 0 && (\n              <div>\n                <h3 className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2\">\n                  On This {selectedElementId ? 'Element' : 'Spread'}\n                </h3>\n                <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n                  {threadComments.map((comment) => (\n                    <CommentItem\n                      key={comment.id}\n                      comment={comment}\n                      onResolve={() => onResolveComment(comment.id)}\n                      onDelete={() => onDeleteComment(comment.id)}\n                      onClick={() => onCommentClick(comment)}\n                    />\n                  ))}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            )}\n\n            {/* General Comments */}\n            {generalComments.length > 0 && (\n              <div>\n                {threadComments.length > 0 && (\n                  <h3 className=\"text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 mt-4\">\n                    Other Comments\n                  </h3>\n                )}\n                <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n                  {generalComments.map((comment) => (\n                    <CommentItem\n                      key={comment.id}\n                      comment={comment}\n                      onResolve={() => onResolveComment(comment.id)}\n                      onDelete={() => onDeleteComment(comment.id)}\n                      onClick={() => onCommentClick(comment)}\n                    />\n                  ))}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default CommentPanel;\n"

---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Component\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport const TemplatePickerPanel: React.FC<TemplatePickerPanelProps> = ({\n  templates: propTemplates,\n  currentAlbumType,\n  isLoading,\n  onApplyTemplate,\n  onApplySpreadTemplate,\n  onClose,\n  className = '',\n}) => {\n  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>('album');\n  const [selectedType, setSelectedType] = useState<AlbumType | 'all'>(\n    currentAlbumType || 'all'\n  );\n  const [previewTemplate, setPreviewTemplate] = useState<AlbumTemplate | null>(null);\n  const [previewSpread, setPreviewSpread] = useState<SpreadTemplate | null>(null);\n\n  // Merge prop templates with defaults\n  const templates = useMemo(() => {\n    if (propTemplates.length > 0) return propTemplates;\n    return DEFAULT_TEMPLATES;\n  }, [propTemplates]);\n\n  // Filter templates by album type\n  const filteredTemplates = useMemo(() => {\n    if (selectedType === 'all') return templates;\n    return templates.filter((t) => t.album_type === selectedType);\n  }, [templates, selectedType]);\n\n  // Get all spread templates from all templates\n  const allSpreadTemplates = useMemo(() => {\n    const spreads: Array<SpreadTemplate & { albumName: string }> = [];\n    templates.forEach((template) => {\n      template.spread_templates.forEach((spread) => {\n        spreads.push({ ...spread, albumName: template.name });\n      });\n    });\n    return spreads;\n  }, [templates]);\n\n  // Handle apply template\n  const handleApplyTemplate = (template: AlbumTemplate) => {\n    onApplyTemplate(template);\n    setPreviewTemplate(null);\n  };\n\n  // Handle apply spread template\n  const handleApplySpread = (spread: SpreadTemplate) => {\n    onApplySpreadTemplate(spread);\n    setPreviewSpread(null);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={`flex flex-col h-full bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 border-l border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700 ${className}`}\n    >\n      {/* Header */}\n      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n        <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">Templates</h3>\n        <button\n          onClick={onClose}\n          className=\"p-1 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-400 dark:hover:text-gray-200\"\n          title=\"Close\"\n        >\n          <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n            <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12\" />\n          </svg>\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Tabs */}\n      <div className=\"flex border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setActiveTab('album')}\n          className={`flex-1 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium ${\n            activeTab === 'album'\n              ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400'\n              : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-400'\n          }`}\n        >\n          Album Templates\n        </button>\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setActiveTab('spread')}\n          className={`flex-1 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium ${\n            activeTab === 'spread'\n              ? 'text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400'\n              : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-400'\n          }`}\n        >\n          Spread Layouts\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Album Type Filter (for album tab) */}\n      {activeTab === 'album' && (\n        <div className=\"p-3 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n          <select\n            value={selectedType}\n            onChange={(e) => setSelectedType(e.target.value as AlbumType | 'all')}\n            className=\"w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 dark:border-gray-600 rounded-md bg-white dark:bg-gray-800 text-gray-900 dark:text-white\"\n          >\n            <option value=\"all\">All Types</option>\n            {Object.entries(ALBUM_TYPE_LABELS).map(([value, label]) => (\n              <option key={value} value={value}>\n                {label}\n              </option>\n            ))}\n          </select>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Content */}\n      <div className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3\">\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-center h-32\">\n            <div className=\"animate-spin rounded-full h-8 w-8 border-b-2 border-blue-600\" />\n          </div>\n        ) : activeTab === 'album' ? (\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-1 gap-3\">\n            {filteredTemplates.map((template) => (\n              <TemplateCard\n                key={template.id}\n                template={template}\n                onPreview={() => setPreviewTemplate(template)}\n                onApply={() => handleApplyTemplate(template)}\n              />\n            ))}\n
