</p>\n            )}\n          </div>\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-2\">\n            {allSpreadTemplates.map((spread) => (\n              <SpreadTemplateCard\n                key={spread.id}\n                spread={spread}\n                albumName={spread.albumName}\n                onPreview={() => setPreviewSpread(spread)}\n                onApply={() => handleApplySpread(spread)}\n              />\n            ))}\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Album Template Preview Modal */}\n      {previewTemplate && (\n        <TemplatePreviewModal\n          template={previewTemplate}\n          onClose={() => setPreviewTemplate(null)}\n          onApply={() => handleApplyTemplate(previewTemplate)}\n        />\n      )}\n\n      {/* Spread Preview Modal */}\n      {previewSpread && (\n        <SpreadPreviewModal\n          spread={previewSpread}\n          onClose={() => setPreviewSpread(null)}\n          onApply={() => handleApplySpread(previewSpread)}\n        />\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Template Card\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface TemplateCardProps {\n  template: AlbumTemplate;\n  onPreview: () => void;\n  onApply: () => void;\n}\n\nconst TemplateCard: React.FC<TemplateCardProps> = ({ template, onPreview, onApply }) => {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative group bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n      {/* Thumbnail */}\n      <div className=\"aspect-[4/3] bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700\">\n        {template.thumbnail_url ? (\n          <img\n            src={template.thumbnail_url}\n            alt={template.name}\n            className=\"w-full h-full object-cover\"\n          />\n        ) : (\n          <div className=\"w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center\">\n            <svg\n              className=\"w-12 h-12 text-gray-400\"\n              fill=\"none\"\n              viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\"\n              stroke=\"currentColor\"\n            >\n              <path\n                strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                strokeWidth={1.5}\n                d=\"M4 16l4.586-4.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L16 16m-2-2l1.586-1.586a2 2 0 012.828 0L20 14m-6-6h.01M6 20h12a2 2 0 002-2V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v12a2 2 0 002 2z\"\n              />\n            </svg>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Premium Badge */}\n        {template.is_premium && (\n          <div className=\"absolute top-2 right-2 px-2 py-1 bg-amber-500 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded\">\n            Premium\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {/* Hover Overlay */}\n        <div className=\"absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n          <button\n            onClick={onPreview}\n            className=\"px-3 py-1.5 text-sm bg-white text-gray-900 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-100\"\n          >\n            Preview\n          </button>\n          <button\n            onClick={onApply}\n            className=\"px-3 py-1.5 text-sm bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700\"\n          >\n            Apply\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Info */}\n      <div className=\"p-3\">\n        <h4 className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white text-sm\">\n          {template.name}\n        </h4>\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400 mt-1 line-clamp-2\">\n          {template.description}\n        </p>\n        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 mt-2\">\n          <span className=\"px-2 py-0.5 bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 text-xs rounded\">\n            {ALBUM_TYPE_LABELS[template.album_type]}\n          </span>\n          <span className=\"text-xs text-gray-400\">\n            {template.spread_templates.length} spreads\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Spread Template Card\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface SpreadTemplateCardProps {\n  spread: SpreadTemplate;\n  albumName: string;\n  onPreview: () => void;\n  onApply: () => void;\n}\n\nconst SpreadTemplateCard: React.FC<SpreadTemplateCardProps> = ({\n  spread,\n  albumName,\n  onPreview,\n  onApply,\n}) => {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"relative group bg-gray-50 dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n      {/* Thumbnail */}\n      <div className=\"aspect-[16/9] bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700\">\n        {spread.thumbnail_url ? (\n          <img\n            src={spread.thumbnail_url}\n            alt={spread.name}\n            className=\"w-full h-full object-cover\"\n          />\n        ) : (\n          <SpreadTemplatePreview spread={spread} />\n        )}\n\n        {/* Hover Overlay */}\n        <div className=\"absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity flex items-center justify-center gap-2\">\n          <button\n            onClick={onPreview}\n            className=\"p-1.5 bg-white text-gray-900 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-100\"\n            title=\"Preview\"\n          >\n            <svg className=\"w-4 h-4\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n              <path\n                strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                strokeWidth={2}\n                d=\"M15 12a3 3 0 11-6 0 3 3 0 016 0z\"\n              />\n              <path\n                strokeLinecap=\"round\"\n                strokeLinejoin=\"round\"\n                strokeWidth={2}\n                d=\"M2.458 12C3.732 7.943 7.523 5 12 5c4.478 0 8.268 2.943 9.542 7-1.274 4.057-5.064 7-9.542 7-4.477 0-8.268-2.943-9.542-7z\"\n              />\n            </svg>\n          </button>\n          <button\n            onClick={onApply}\n            className=\"p-1.5 bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700\"\n            title=\"Apply to current spread\"\n          >\n            <svg className=\"w-4 h-4\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n              <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M5 13l4 4L19 7\" />\n            </svg>\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* Info */}\n      <div className=\"p-2\">\n        <h4 className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white text-xs truncate\">\n          {spread.name}\n        </h4>\n        <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-400 truncate\">{albumName}</p>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Spread Template Preview (Mini)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface SpreadTemplatePreviewProps {\n  spread: SpreadTemplate;\n}\n\nconst SpreadTemplatePreview: React.FC<SpreadTemplatePreviewProps> = ({ spread }) => {\n  const width = spread.spread_type === 'full_spread' ? 200 : 100;\n  const height = 66;\n  const scale = 0.02;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-white dark:bg-gray-600\">\n      <svg width={width} height={height} viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`}>\n        {spread.elements.map((el, idx) => {\n          const x = el.x * scale;\n          const y = el.y * scale;\n          const w = el.width * scale;\n          const h = el.height * scale;\n\n          if (el.element_type === 'photo') {\n            return (\n              <rect\n                key={idx}\n                x={x}\n                y={y}\n                width={w}\n                height={h}\n                fill=\"#e5e7eb\"\n                stroke=\"#9ca3af\"\n                strokeWidth={0.5}\n              />\n            );\n          } else if (el.element_type === 'text') {\n            return (\n              <rect\n                key={idx}\n                x={x}\n                y={y}\n                width={w}\n                height={h}\n                fill=\"#dbeafe\"\n                stroke=\"#93c5fd\"\n                strokeWidth={0.5}\n              />\n            );\n          } else if (el.element_type === 'shape') {\n            return (\n              <rect\n                key={idx}\n                x={x}\n                y={y}\n                width={w}\n                height={h}\n                fill=\"#fce7f3\"\n                stroke=\"#f9a8d4\"\n                strokeWidth={0.5}\n                rx={el.style?.border_radius ? (el.style.border_radius * scale) : 0}\n              />\n            );\n          }\n          return null;\n        })}\n      </svg>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Template Preview Modal\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface className=\"bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-2xl w-full mx-4 max-h-[80vh] overflow-hidden\"\n        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}\n      >\n        {/* Header */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n          <div>\n            <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-lg text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n              {template.name}\n            </h3>\n            <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">\n              {template.description}\n            </p>\n          </div>\n          <button\n            onClick={onClose}\n            className=\"p-1 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-400\"\n          >\n            <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n              <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12\" />\n            </svg>\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Content */}\n        <div className=\"p-6 overflow-y-auto max-h-[50vh]\">\n          {/* Main Image */}\n          <div className=\"aspect-[4/3] bg-gray-200 dark:bg-gray-700 rounded-lg mb-4\">\n            {template.thumbnail_url ? (\n              <img\n                src={template.thumbnail_url}\n                alt={template.name}\n                className=\"w-full h-full object-cover rounded-lg\"\n              />\n            ) : (\n              <div className=\"w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center\">\n                <span className=\"text-gray-400\">No preview available</span>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Details */}\n          <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 text-sm\">\n            <div>\n              <span className=\"text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">Album Type</span>\n              <p className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n                {ALBUM_TYPE_LABELS[template.album_type]}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n            <div>\n              <span className=\"text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">Default Size</span>\n              <p className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n                {template.default_width} × {template.default_height} px\n              </p>\n            </div>\n            <div>\n              <span className=\"text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">Spread Templates</span>\n              <p className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n                {template.spread_templates.length} layouts included\n              </p>\n            </div>\n            <div>\n              <span className=\"text-gray-500 dark:text-gray-400\">Tier</span>\n              <p className=\"font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">\n                {template.is_premium ? 'Premium' : 'Free'}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* Spread Previews */}\n          <div className=\"mt-4\">\n            <h4 className=\"text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 dark:text-white mb-2\">\n              Included Spreads\n            </h4>\n            <div className=\"flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2\">\n              {template.spread_templates.map((spread) => (\n                <div\n                  key={spread.id}\n                  className=\"flex-shrink-0 w-32 bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 rounded p-2\"\n                >\n                  <SpreadTemplatePreview spread={spread} />\n                  <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300 mt-1 text-center truncate\">\n                    {spread.name}\n                  </p>\n                </div>\n              ))}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Footer */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-3 px-6 py-4 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n          <button\n            onClick={onClose}\n            className=\"px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 rounded-md\"\n          >\n            Cancel\n          </button>\n          <button\n            onClick={onApply}\n            className=\"px-4 py-2 text-sm bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700\"\n          >\n            Apply Template\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Spread Preview Modal\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface SpreadPreviewModalProps {\n  spread: SpreadTemplate;\n  onClose: () => void;\n  onApply: () => void;\n}\n\nconst SpreadPreviewModal: React.FC<SpreadPreviewModalProps> = ({\n  spread,\n  onClose,\n  onApply,\n}) => {\n  return (\n    <div\n      className=\"fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60\"\n      onClick={onClose}\n    >\n      <div\n        className=\"bg-white dark:bg-gray-900 rounded-lg shadow-xl max-w-md w-full mx-4\"\n        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}\n      >\n        {/* Header */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n          <h3 className=\"font-semibold text-gray-900 dark:text-white\">{spread.name}</h3>\n          <button\n            onClick={onClose}\n            className=\"p-1 text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-400\"\n          >\n            <svg className=\"w-5 h-5\" fill=\"none\" viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\" stroke=\"currentColor\">\n              <path strokeLinecap=\"round\" strokeLinejoin=\"round\" strokeWidth={2} d=\"M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12\" />\n            </svg>\n          </button>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Preview */}\n        <div className=\"p-6\">\n          <div className=\"aspect-[16/9] bg-gray-100 dark:bg-gray-800 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center\">\n            <svg\n              width=\"100%\"\n              height=\"100%\"\n              viewBox={`0 0 ${spread.spread_type === 'full_spread' ? 400 : 200} 133`}\n              preserveAspectRatio=\"xMidYMid meet\"\n            >\n              <rect\n                x=\"0\"\n                y=\"0\"\n                width={spread.spread_type === 'full_spread' ? 400 : 200}\n                height=\"133\"\n                fill=\"white\"\n              />\n              {spread.elements.map((el, idx) => {\n                const scale = 0.04;\n                const x = el.x * scale;\n                const y = el.y * scale;\n                const w = el.width * scale;\n                const h = el.height * scale;\n\n                if (el.element_type === 'photo') {\n                  return (\n                    <g key={idx}>\n                      <rect\n                        x={x}\n                        y={y}\n                        width={w}\n                        height={h}\n                        fill=\"#e5e7eb\"\n                        stroke=\"#9ca3af\"\n                        strokeWidth={1}\n                      />\n                      <line x1={x} y1={y} x2={x + w} y2={y + h} stroke=\"#9ca3af\" strokeWidth={0.5} />\n                      <line x1={x + w} y1={y} x2={x} y2={y + h} stroke=\"#9ca3af\" strokeWidth={0.5} />\n                    </g>\n                  );\n                } else if (el.element_type === 'text') {\n                  return (\n                    <rect\n                      key={idx}\n                      x={x}\n                      y={y}\n                      width={w}\n                      height={h}\n                      fill=\"#dbeafe\"\n                      stroke=\"#3b82f6\"\n                      strokeWidth={1}\n                    />\n                  );\n                } else if (el.element_type === 'shape') {\n                  return (\n                    <rect\n                      key={idx}\n                      x={x}\n                      y={y}\n                      width={w}\n                      height={h}\n                      fill={el.style?.fill_color || '#fce7f3'}\n                      stroke=\"#ec4899\"\n                      strokeWidth={1}\n                      rx={el.style?.border_radius ? (el.style.border_radius * scale) : 0}\n                    />\n                  );\n                }\n                return null;\n              })}\n            </svg>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"mt-4 text-sm text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300\">\n            <p>\n              <span className=\"font-medium\">Type:</span>{' '}\n              {spread.spread_type === 'full_spread' ? 'Full Spread (2 pages)' : 'Single Page'}\n            </p>\n            <p>\n              <span className=\"font-medium\">Elements:</span> {spread.elements.length} placeholders\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Footer */}\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-t border-gray-200 dark:border-gray-700\">\n          <button\n            onClick={onClose}\n            className=\"px-4 py-2 text-sm text-gray-700 dark:text-gray-300 hover:bg-gray-100 dark:hover:bg-gray-800 rounded-md\"\n          >\n            Cancel\n          </button>\n          <button\n            onClick={onApply}\n            className=\"px-4 py-2 text-sm bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-md hover:bg-blue-700\"\n          >\n            Apply to Current Spread\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default TemplatePickerPanel;\n"

  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\admin\\Desktop\\RawDrive2\\frontend\\src\\hooks\\useAlbumTemplates.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * useAlbumTemplates Hook\n *\n * Fetches and manages album templates for the album design editor.\n * Handles caching, filtering by album type, and applying templates.\n */\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport type { AlbumTemplate, AlbumType, SpreadTemplate } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface UseAlbumTemplatesOptions {\n  /** Workspace ID */\n  workspaceId: string;\n  /** Auto-fetch templates on mount */\n  autoFetch?: boolean;\n  /** Filter by album type */\n  albumType?: AlbumType;\n}\n\ninterface UseAlbumTemplatesReturn {\n  /** All templates */\n  templates: AlbumTemplate[];\n  /** Templates filtered by current album type */\n  filteredTemplates: AlbumTemplate[];\n  /** Whether templates are loading */\n  isLoading: boolean;\n  /** Error message if fetch failed */\n  error: string | null;\n  /** Refresh templates from API */\n  refreshTemplates: () => Promise<void>;\n  /** Get template by ID */\n  getTemplate: (templateId: string) => AlbumTemplate | undefined;\n  /** Get spread template by ID */\n  getSpreadTemplate: (templateId: string, spreadId: string) => SpreadTemplate | undefined;\n  /** Get all spread templates across all album templates */\n  getAllSpreadTemplates: () => SpreadTemplate[];\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Hook\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport function useAlbumTemplates({\n  workspaceId,\n  autoFetch = true,\n  albumType,\n}: UseAlbumTemplatesOptions): UseAlbumTemplatesReturn {\n  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<AlbumTemplate[]>([]);\n  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Fetch templates from API\n  const fetchTemplates = useCallback(async () => {\n    setIsLoading(true);\n    setError(null);\n\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(\n        `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/templates`,\n        {\n          method: 'GET',\n          headers: {\n            'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n          },\n        }\n      );\n\n      if (!response.ok) {\n        throw new Error('Failed to fetch templates');\n      }\n\n      const data = await response.json();\n      setTemplates(data.templates || []);\n    } catch (err) {\n      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to fetch templates';\n      setError(message);\n      console.error('Error fetching album templates:', err);\n    } finally {\n      setIsLoading(false);\n    }\n  }, [workspaceId]);\n\n  // Filter templates by album type\n  const filteredTemplates = albumType\n    ? templates.filter((t) => t.album_type === albumType)\n    : templates;\n\n  // Get template by ID\n  const getTemplate = useCallback(\n    (templateId: string) => {\n      return templates.find((t) => t.id === templateId);\n    },\n    [templates]\n  );\n\n  // Get spread template by ID\n  const getSpreadTemplate = useCallback(\n    (templateId: string, spreadId: string) => {\n      const template = templates.find((t) => t.id === templateId);\n      return template?.spread_templates.find((s) => s.id === spreadId);\n    },\n    [templates]\n  );\n\n  // Get all spread templates\n  const getAllSpreadTemplates = useCallback(() => {\n    const spreads: SpreadTemplate[] = [];\n    templates.forEach((template) => {\n      spreads.push(...template.spread_templates);\n    });\n    return spreads;\n  }, [templates]);\n\n  // Auto-fetch on mount\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (autoFetch) {\n      fetchTemplates();\n    }\n  }, [autoFetch, fetchTemplates]);\n\n  return {\n    templates,\n    filteredTemplates,\n    isLoading,\n    error,\n    refreshTemplates: fetchTemplates,\n    getTemplate,\n    getSpreadTemplate,\n    getAllSpreadTemplates,\n  };\n}\n\nexport default useAlbumTemplates;\n"
}

useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport type { AlbumComment, MentionedUser, CommentStatus } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface UseAlbumCommentsOptions {\n  /** Album ID */\n  albumId: string;\n  /** Workspace ID */\n  workspaceId: string;\n  /** Auto-fetch comments on mount */\n  autoFetch?: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface UseAlbumCommentsReturn {\n  /** All comments */\n  comments: AlbumComment[];\n  /** Open (unresolved) comments */\n  openComments: AlbumComment[];\n  /** Resolved comments */\n  resolvedComments: AlbumComment[];\n  /** Whether comments are loading */\n  isLoading: boolean;\n  /** Error message */\n  error: string | null;\n  /** Refresh comments from API */\n  refreshComments: () => Promise<void>;\n  /** Create a new comment */\n  createComment: (\n    content: string,\n    options: {\n      spreadId?: string;\n      elementId?: string;\n      positionX?: number;\n      positionY?: number;\n      mentionedUserIds?: string[];\n      parentCommentId?: string;\n    }\n  ) => Promise<AlbumComment | null>;\n  /** Update a comment */\n  updateComment: (commentId: string, content: string) => Promise<boolean>;\n  /** Delete a comment */\n  deleteComment: (commentId: string) => Promise<boolean>;\n  /** Resolve a comment */\n  resolveComment: (commentId: string) => Promise<boolean>;\n  /** Reopen a resolved comment */\n  reopenComment: (commentId: string) => Promise<boolean>;\n  /** Get comments for a specific spread */\n  getCommentsForSpread: (spreadId: string) => AlbumComment[];\n  /** Get comments for a specific element */\n  getCommentsForElement: (elementId: string) => AlbumComment[];\n  /** Available users for @mentions */\n  availableUsers: MentionedUser[];\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Hook\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport function useAlbumComments({\n  albumId,\n  workspaceId,\n  autoFetch = true,\n}: UseAlbumCommentsOptions): UseAlbumCommentsReturn {\n  const [comments, setComments] = useState<AlbumComment[]>([]);\n  const [availableUsers, setAvailableUsers] = useState<MentionedUser[]>([]);\n  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Fetch comments from API\n  const fetchComments = useCallback(async () => {\n    setIsLoading(true);\n    setError(null);\n\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(\n        `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/comments`,\n        {\n          method: 'GET',\n          headers: {\n            'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n          },\n        }\n      );\n\n      if (!response.ok) {\n        throw new Error('Failed to fetch comments');\n      }\n\n      const data = await response.json();\n      setComments(data.comments || []);\n    } catch (err) {\n      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to fetch comments';\n      setError(message);\n      console.error('Error fetching album comments:', err);\n    } finally {\n      setIsLoading(false);\n    }\n  }, [albumId, workspaceId]);\n\n  // Fetch available users for @mentions\n  const fetchAvailableUsers = useCallback(async () => {\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(\n        `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/members`,\n        {\n          method: 'GET',\n          headers: {\n            'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n          },\n        }\n      );\n\n      if (!response.ok) return;\n\n      const data = await response.json();\n      const users: MentionedUser[] = (data.members || []).map((member: {\n        id: string;\n        user: { display_name: string; avatar_url?: string };\n      }) => ({\n        id: member.id,\n        display_name: member.user?.display_name || 'Unknown',\n        avatar_url: member.user?.avatar_url,\n      }));\n      setAvailableUsers(users);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('Error fetching workspace members:', err);\n    }\n  }, [workspaceId]);\n\n  // Create a new comment\n  const createComment = useCallback(\n    async (\n      content: string,\n      options: {\n        spreadId?: string;\n        elementId?: string;\n        positionX?: number;\n        positionY?: number;\n        mentionedUserIds?: string[];\n        parentCommentId?: string;\n      }\n    ): Promise<AlbumComment | null> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/comments`,\n          {\n            method: 'POST',\n            headers: {\n              'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n            },\n            body: JSON.stringify({\n              content,\n              spread_id: options.spreadId,\n              element_id: options.elementId,\n              position_x: options.positionX,\n              position_y: options.positionY,\n              mentioned_user_ids: options.mentionedUserIds || [],\n              parent_comment_id: options.parentCommentId,\n            }),\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to create comment');\n        }\n\n        const newComment = await response.json();\n        setComments((prev) => [...prev, newComment]);\n        return newComment;\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error creating comment:', err);\n        return null;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId]\n  );\n\n  // Update a comment\n  const updateComment = useCallback(\n    async (commentId: string, content: string): Promise<boolean> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/comments/${commentId}`,\n          {\n            method: 'PATCH',\n            headers: {\n              'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n            },\n            body: JSON.stringify({ content }),\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to update comment');\n        }\n\n        const updatedComment = await response.json();\n        setComments((prev) =>\n          prev.map((c) => (c.id === commentId ? updatedComment : c))\n        );\n        return true;\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error updating comment:', err);\n        return false;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId]\n  );\n\n  // Delete a comment\n  const deleteComment = useCallback(\n    async (commentId: string): Promise<boolean> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/comments/${commentId}`,\n          {\n            method: 'DELETE',\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to delete comment');\n        }\n\n        setComments((prev) => prev.filter((c) => c.id !== commentId));\n        return true;\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error deleting comment:', err);\n        return false;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId]\n  );\n\n  // Resolve a comment\n  const resolveComment = useCallback(\n    async (commentId: string): Promise<boolean> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/comments/${commentId}/resolve`,\n          {\n            method: 'POST',\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to resolve comment');\n        }\n\n        const resolvedComment = await response.json();\n        setComments((prev) =>\n          prev.map((c) => (c.id === commentId ? resolvedComment : c))\n        );\n        return true;\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error resolving comment:', err);\n        return false;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId]\n  );\n\n  // Reopen a resolved comment\n  const reopenComment = useCallback(\n    async (commentId: string): Promise<boolean> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/comments/${commentId}/reopen`,\n          {\n            method: 'POST',\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to reopen comment');\n        }\n\n        const reopenedComment = await response.json();\n        setComments((prev) =>\n          prev.map((c) => (c.id === commentId ? reopenedComment : c))\n        );\n        return true;\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error reopening comment:', err);\n        return false;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId]\n  );\n\n  // Get comments for a specific spread\n  const getCommentsForSpread = useCallback(\n    (spreadId: string): AlbumComment[] => {\n      return comments.filter((c) => c.spread_id === spreadId);\n    },\n    [comments]\n  );\n\n  // Get comments for a specific element\n  const getCommentsForElement = useCallback(\n    (elementId: string): AlbumComment[] => {\n      return comments.filter((c) => c.element_id === elementId);\n    },\n    [comments]\n  );\n\n  // Filter comments by status\n  const openComments = comments.filter((c) => c.status === 'open');\n  const resolvedComments = comments.filter((c) => c.status === 'resolved');\n\n  // Listen for WebSocket comment events\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleCommentEvent = (event: CustomEvent) => {\n      const { type, comment } = event.detail;\n\n      switch (type) {\n        case 'album:comment_added':\n          setComments((prev) => {\n            // Avoid duplicates\n            if (prev.some((c) => c.id === comment.id)) return prev;\n            return [...prev, comment];\n          });\n          break;\n\n        case 'album:comment_resolved':\n          setComments((prev) =>\n            prev.map((c) => (c.id === comment.id ? { ...c, status: 'resolved' as CommentStatus } : c))\n          );\n          break;\n\n        case 'album:comment_deleted':\n          setComments((prev) => prev.filter((c) => c.id !== comment.id));\n          break;\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('album:comment', handleCommentEvent as EventListener);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('album:comment', handleCommentEvent as EventListener);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // Auto-fetch on mount\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (autoFetch) {\n      fetchComments();\n      fetchAvailableUsers();\n    }\n  }, [autoFetch, fetchComments, fetchAvailableUsers]);\n\n  return {\n    comments,\n    openComments,\n    resolvedComments,\n    isLoading,\n    error,\n    refreshComments: fetchComments,\n    createComment,\n    updateComment,\n    deleteComment,\n    resolveComment,\n    reopenComment,\n    getCommentsForSpread,\n    getCommentsForElement,\n    availableUsers,\n  };\n}\n\nexport default useAlbumComments;\n"
}

  "content": "/**\n * useAlbumVersionHistory Hook\n *\n * Manages album version history with snapshot creation and rollback capabilities.\n * Listens to WebSocket events for real-time version updates.\n */\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport type { AlbumVersion, AlbumSnapshot } from '@rawdrive/shared-types';\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ninterface UseAlbumVersionHistoryOptions {\n  /** Album ID */\n  albumId: string;\n  /** Workspace ID */\n  workspaceId: string;\n  /** Auto-fetch versions on mount */\n  autoFetch?: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface UseAlbumVersionHistoryReturn {\n  /** All versions */\n  versions: AlbumVersion[];\n  /** Whether versions are loading */\n  isLoading: boolean;\n  /** Error message */\n  error: string | null;\n  /** Refresh versions from API */\n  refreshVersions: () => Promise<void>;\n  /** Create a named snapshot */\n  createSnapshot: (name: string, description?: string) => Promise<AlbumVersion | null>;\n  /** Rollback to a specific version */\n  rollbackToVersion: (versionId: string) => Promise<boolean>;\n  /** Get version by ID */\n  getVersion: (versionId: string) => AlbumVersion | undefined;\n  /** Preview a version (returns snapshot data) */\n  previewVersion: (versionId: string) => Promise<AlbumSnapshot | null>;\n  /** Compare two versions */\n  compareVersions: (\n    versionId1: string,\n    versionId2: string\n  ) => Promise<{ added: string[]; removed: string[]; modified: string[] } | null>;\n  /** Current version (most recent) */\n  currentVersion: AlbumVersion | null;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Hook\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport function useAlbumVersionHistory({\n  albumId,\n  workspaceId,\n  autoFetch = true,\n}: UseAlbumVersionHistoryOptions): UseAlbumVersionHistoryReturn {\n  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<AlbumVersion[]>([]);\n  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Fetch versions from API\n  const fetchVersions = useCallback(async () => {\n    setIsLoading(true);\n    setError(null);\n\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(\n        `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/versions`,\n        {\n          method: 'GET',\n          headers: {\n            'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n          },\n        }\n      );\n\n      if (!response.ok) {\n        throw new Error('Failed to fetch versions');\n      }\n\n      const data = await response.json();\n      // Sort by version number descending (most recent first)\n      const sortedVersions = (data.versions || []).sort(\n        (a: AlbumVersion, b: AlbumVersion) => b.version_number - a.version_number\n      );\n      setVersions(sortedVersions);\n    } catch (err) {\n      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to fetch versions';\n      setError(message);\n      console.error('Error fetching album versions:', err);\n    } finally {\n      setIsLoading(false);\n    }\n  }, [albumId, workspaceId]);\n\n  // Create a named snapshot\n  const createSnapshot = useCallback(\n    async (name: string, description?: string): Promise<AlbumVersion | null> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/versions`,\n          {\n            method: 'POST',\n            headers: {\n              'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n            },\n            body: JSON.stringify({\n              name,\n              description,\n              is_named_snapshot: true,\n            }),\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to create snapshot');\n        }\n\n        const newVersion = await response.json();\n        setVersions((prev) => [newVersion, ...prev]);\n        return newVersion;\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error creating snapshot:', err);\n        return null;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId]\n  );\n\n  // Rollback to a specific version\n  const rollbackToVersion = useCallback(\n    async (versionId: string): Promise<boolean> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/versions/${versionId}/rollback`,\n          {\n            method: 'POST',\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to rollback to version');\n        }\n\n        // Refresh versions after rollback\n        await fetchVersions();\n        return true;\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error rolling back to version:', err);\n        return false;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId, fetchVersions]\n  );\n\n  // Get version by ID\n  const getVersion = useCallback(\n    (versionId: string): AlbumVersion | undefined => {\n      return versions.find((v) => v.id === versionId);\n    },\n    [versions]\n  );\n\n  // Preview a version\n  const previewVersion = useCallback(\n    async (versionId: string): Promise<AlbumSnapshot | null> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/versions/${versionId}`,\n          {\n            method: 'GET',\n            headers: {\n              'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n            },\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to fetch version snapshot');\n        }\n\n        const data = await response.json();\n        return data.snapshot || null;\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error previewing version:', err);\n        return null;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId]\n  );\n\n  // Compare two versions\n  const compareVersions = useCallback(\n    async (\n      versionId1: string,\n      versionId2: string\n    ): Promise<{ added: string[]; removed: string[]; modified: string[] } | null> => {\n      try {\n        const response = await fetch(\n          `/api/v1/workspaces/${workspaceId}/albums/${albumId}/versions/compare?` +\n            `version1=${versionId1}&version2=${versionId2}`,\n          {\n            method: 'GET',\n            headers: {\n              'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n            },\n          }\n        );\n\n        if (!response.ok) {\n          throw new Error('Failed to compare versions');\n        }\n\n        return await response.json();\n      } catch (err) {\n        console.error('Error comparing versions:', err);\n        return null;\n      }\n    },\n    [albumId, workspaceId]\n  );\n\n  // Get current version (most recent)\n  const currentVersion = versions.length > 0 ? versions[0] : null;\n\n  // Listen for WebSocket version events\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleVersionEvent = (event: CustomEvent) => {\n      const { type, version } = event.detail;\n\n      switch (type) {\n        case 'album:version_created':\n          setVersions((prev) => {\n            // Avoid duplicates\n            if (prev.some((v) => v.id === version.id)) return prev;\n            return [version, ...prev];\n          });\n          break;\n\n        case 'album:version_restored':\n          // Refresh the full list after a rollback\n          fetchVersions();\n          break;\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('album:version', handleVersionEvent as EventListener);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('album:version', handleVersionEvent as EventListener);\n    };\n  }, [fetchVersions]);\n\n  // Auto-fetch on mount\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (autoFetch) {\n      fetchVersions();\n    }\n  }, [autoFetch, fetchVersions]);\n\n  return {\n    versions,\n    isLoading,\n    error,\n    refreshVersions: fetchVersions,\n    createSnapshot,\n    rollbackToVersion,\n    getVersion,\n    previewVersion,\n    compareVersions,\n    currentVersion,\n  };\n}\n\nexport default useAlbumVersionHistory;\n"
}

The implementation of the collaborative album design feature is now complete. Here's a summary of what was implemented:

## Feature Implementation Summary: Real-time Collaborative Album Design

### Backend (Previous Session)
- **Migration**: `0188_collaborative_album_design.py` - Database schema for albums, spreads, elements, sessions, versions, and comments
- **Service**: `album_collaboration_service.py` - CRDT-based collaboration with Lamport timestamps and vector clocks
- **API Endpoints**: `album_collaboration.py` - Full REST API for album operations
- **WebSocket**: Extended `websocket_service.py` with album collaboration events

### Frontend Components (`frontend/src/components/features/album-design/`)

| Component | Purpose |
|-----------|---------|
| `AlbumEditor.tsx` | Main editor orchestrating all sub-components |
| `SpreadCanvas.tsx` | Canvas for spread editing with drag/resize |
| `SpreadElement.tsx` | Individual element rendering (photo/text/shape) |
| `SpreadThumbnailStrip.tsx` | Navigation strip with drag-to-reorder |
| `AlbumToolbar.tsx` | Tools and controls toolbar |
| `ElementPropertiesPanel.tsx` | Properties editing panel |
| `AlbumCollaborationBar.tsx` | Collaboration status display |
| `CollaboratorCursor.tsx` | Remote user cursor display |
| `SelectionBox.tsx` | Multi-select rectangle |
| `VersionHistoryPanel.tsx` | Version history with rollback |
| `CommentPanel.tsx` | Comments with @mentions support |
| `TemplatePickerPanel.tsx` | Album and spread template browser |

### Frontend Hooks (`frontend/src/hooks/`)

| Hook | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `useAlbumCollaboration.ts` | WebSocket connection, CRDT operations, presence tracking |
| `useAlbumTemplates.ts` | Template fetching and filtering |
| `useAlbumComments.ts` | Comment CRUD with real-time updates |
| `useAlbumVersionHistory.ts` | Version management and rollback |

### Shared Types (`packages/shared-types/src/album-collaboration.ts`)
- Complete TypeScript definitions matching backend models
- Enums for album status, types, elements, operations
- Interfaces for albums, spreads, elements, sessions, versions, comments
- WebSocket message type definitions

### Key Features Implemented
1. **Real-time Collaboration**: Multiple users can edit simultaneously via WebSocket
2. **CRDT Support**: Conflict-free editing using Lamport timestamps and vector clocks
3. **Presence Indicators**: See who's editing and their cursor positions
4. **Version History**: Create snapshots and rollback to previous versions
5. **Comments with @mentions**: Team feedback on spreads/elements
6. **Template System**: Pre-built album and spread layouts for 6 album types (wedding, portrait, events, family, newborn, corporate)
7. **Undo/Redo**: Full operation history with keyboard shortcuts
